# FILE: functions/fn_calc_line_price_draft.deluge
================================================================================
Map fn_calc_line_price_draft(string p_part_number, decimal p_qty, string p_description, string p_customer_type, string p_currency, decimal p_markup_pct, string p_fx_mode)
{
	result = Map();
	qty_raw = ifnull(p_qty,0);
	qty_rounded = qty_raw.round(0);
	qty_long = qty_rounded.toLong();

	eur_usd = 0.0;
	eur_usd_found = false;
	for each rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == "EUR"]
	{
		raw_eur_per_usd = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
		if(raw_eur_per_usd != 0)
		{
			eur_usd = 1 / raw_eur_per_usd;
			eur_usd_found = true;
		}
		break;
	}

	currency = ifnull(p_currency,"USD");
	fx_mode = ifnull(p_fx_mode,"non_usd_only");
	apply_fx = false;
	fx_rate = 1.0;

	fx_mode_always = false;
	if(fx_mode == "always")
	{
		fx_mode_always = true;
	}

	fx_mode_non_usd_only = false;
	if(fx_mode == "non_usd_only")
	{
		fx_mode_non_usd_only = true;
	}

	currency_not_usd = false;
	if(currency != "USD")
	{
		currency_not_usd = true;
	}

	if(fx_mode_always)
	{
		apply_fx = true;
	}
	if(fx_mode_non_usd_only)
	{
		if(currency_not_usd)
		{
			apply_fx = true;
		}
	}

	fx_rate_found = false;
	if(apply_fx)
	{
		for each rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == currency]
		{
			fx_rate = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
			break;
		}
		if(fx_rate != 0)
		{
			fx_rate_found = true;
		}
	}

	fx_missing = false;
	if(apply_fx)
	{
		if(fx_rate_found == false)
		{
			fx_missing = true;
		}
	}

	rate_missing = false;
	if(fx_missing)
	{
		rate_missing = true;
	}
	if(eur_usd_found == false)
	{
		rate_missing = true;
	}

	missing_label = currency;
	if(eur_usd_found == false)
	{
		missing_label = "EUR";
	}

	eur_price = thisapp.fn_get_tier_price(p_part_number, qty_long);

	price_found = true;
	if(eur_price < 0)
	{
		price_found = false;
	}

	if(price_found == false)
	{
		eur_price = 0;
	}

	discount = 0.0;
	discountable = "N";
	item_description = ifnull(p_description,"");
	customer_type = ifnull(p_customer_type,"End Customer");

	for each item in Item_Master[Part_Number == p_part_number]
	{
		discountable = ifnull(item.Discountable,"N");
		is_discountable = false;
		if(discountable == "Y")
		{
			is_discountable = true;
		}

		category_val = ifnull(item.Category,"");
		is_software = false;
		if(category_val == "Software")
		{
			is_software = true;
		}

		if(is_discountable)
		{
			discount = thisapp.fn_get_discount(customer_type, qty_long, is_software);
		}

		item_description_from_master = ifnull(item.Description, item_description);
		item_description = item_description_from_master;
		break;
	}

	markup_raw = ifnull(p_markup_pct,0);
	markup = markup_raw / 100;
	usd_cost = eur_price * eur_usd;
	discount_factor = 1 - discount;
	markup_factor = 1 + markup;
	unit_price_step1 = usd_cost * discount_factor;
	unit_price_usd = unit_price_step1 * markup_factor;

	qty_for_total = ifnull(p_qty,0);
	line_total_usd = unit_price_usd * qty_for_total;
	fx_unit_price = unit_price_usd;
	line_total_fx = line_total_usd;

	if(apply_fx)
	{
		if(fx_rate_found)
		{
			fx_unit_price = unit_price_usd * fx_rate;
			line_total_fx = line_total_usd * fx_rate;
		}
	}

	if(fx_missing)
	{
		fx_unit_price = 0;
		line_total_fx = 0;
	}

	if(eur_usd_found == false)
	{
		unit_price_usd = 0;
		line_total_usd = 0;
		fx_unit_price = 0;
		line_total_fx = 0;
	}

	description = item_description;
	if(price_found == false)
	{
		description = "[NO PRICE ON FILE - DO NOT QUOTE] " + description;
	}

	if(rate_missing)
	{
		has_fx_marker = description.contains("[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE");
		if(has_fx_marker == false)
		{
			description = "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE FOR " + missing_label + " - DO NOT QUOTE] " + description;
		}
	}

	result.put("unit_price", unit_price_usd.round(2));
	result.put("fx_unit_price", fx_unit_price.round(2));
	result.put("line_total_usd", line_total_usd.round(2));
	result.put("line_total_fx", line_total_fx.round(2));
	result.put("description", description);
	result.put("discount", discount);
	result.put("discountable", discountable);
	result.put("price_found", price_found);
	return result;
}

# FILE: functions/fn_calc_quote_lines.deluge
================================================================================
void fn_calc_quote_lines(string p_quote_id)
{
	quote_id_long = p_quote_id.toLong();
	for each quote in Quote_Request
	{
		if(quote.ID == quote_id_long)
		{
			markup_raw = ifnull(quote.Markup_Rate_Pct,0);
			markup = markup_raw / 100;
			customer_type = ifnull(quote.Customer_Type,"End Customer");
			currency = ifnull(quote.Currency,"USD");
			fx_mode = ifnull(quote.FX_Charge_Mode,"non_usd_only");
			fx_rate = 1.0;
			eur_usd = 0.0;
			eur_usd_found = false;
			apply_fx = false;

			for each rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == "EUR"]
			{
				raw_eur_per_usd = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
				if(raw_eur_per_usd != 0)
				{
					eur_usd = 1 / raw_eur_per_usd;
					eur_usd_found = true;
				}
				break;
			}

			quote.EUR_USD_Rate = eur_usd;

			if(fx_mode == "always")
			{
				apply_fx = true;
			}
			if(fx_mode == "non_usd_only")
			{
				if(currency != "USD")
				{
					apply_fx = true;
				}
			}

			fx_rate_found = false;
			if(apply_fx)
			{
				for each rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == currency]
				{
					fx_rate = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
					break;
				}
				if(fx_rate != 0)
				{
					fx_rate_found = true;
				}
			}

			fx_missing = false;
			if(apply_fx)
			{
				if(fx_rate_found == false)
				{
					fx_missing = true;
				}
			}

			rate_missing = false;
			if(fx_missing)
			{
				rate_missing = true;
			}
			if(eur_usd_found == false)
			{
				rate_missing = true;
			}

			missing_label = currency;
			if(eur_usd_found == false)
			{
				missing_label = "EUR";
			}

			line_seq = 0;
			for each line in quote.Quote_Lines
			{
				line_seq = line_seq + 1;
				line.Line_Number = line_seq;

				qty_raw = ifnull(line.Qty,0);
				qty = qty_raw.toLong();
				part = ifnull(line.Part_Number,"");

				part_is_blank = false;
				if(part == "")
				{
					part_is_blank = true;
				}
				if(part == "null")
				{
					part_is_blank = true;
				}

				if(part_is_blank)
				{
					if(line.Part_Select != null)
					{
						select_part_raw = ifnull(line.Part_Select.Part_Number,"");
						part = select_part_raw.toString();

						part_is_usable = true;
						if(part == "")
						{
							part_is_usable = false;
						}
						if(part == "null")
						{
							part_is_usable = false;
						}

						if(part_is_usable)
						{
							line.Part_Number = part;
						}
					}
				}

				eur_price = thisapp.fn_get_tier_price(part,qty);

				price_found = true;
				if(eur_price < 0)
				{
					price_found = false;
				}

				if(price_found == false)
				{
					eur_price = 0;
				}

				usd_cost = eur_price * eur_usd;
				discount = 0.0;

				for each item in Item_Master[Part_Number == part]
				{
					discountable_val = ifnull(item.Discountable,"N");

					category_val = ifnull(item.Category,"");
					is_software = false;
					if(category_val == "Software")
					{
						is_software = true;
					}

					if(discountable_val == "Y")
					{
						discount = thisapp.fn_get_discount(customer_type,qty,is_software);
					}

					line.Description = ifnull(item.Description,"");
					break;
				}

				if(price_found == false)
				{
					old_description = ifnull(line.Description,"");
					line.Description = "[NO PRICE ON FILE - DO NOT QUOTE] " + old_description;
				}

				discount_factor = 1 - discount;
				markup_factor = 1 + markup;
				unit_price_step1 = usd_cost * discount_factor;
				unit_price_usd = unit_price_step1 * markup_factor;

				line_total_usd = unit_price_usd * qty;
				fx_unit_price = unit_price_usd;
				line_total_fx = line_total_usd;

				if(apply_fx)
				{
					if(fx_rate_found)
					{
						fx_unit_price = unit_price_usd * fx_rate;
						line_total_fx = line_total_usd * fx_rate;
					}
				}

				if(fx_missing)
				{
					fx_unit_price = 0;
					line_total_fx = 0;
				}

				if(eur_usd_found == false)
				{
					unit_price_usd = 0;
					line_total_usd = 0;
					fx_unit_price = 0;
					line_total_fx = 0;
				}

				if(rate_missing)
				{
					desc_now = ifnull(line.Description,"");
					has_fx_marker = desc_now.contains("[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE");
					if(has_fx_marker == false)
					{
						line.Description = "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE FOR " + missing_label + " - DO NOT QUOTE] " + desc_now;
					}
				}

				line.Unit_Price = unit_price_usd.round(2);
				line.FX_Unit_Price = fx_unit_price.round(2);
				line.Line_Total_USD = line_total_usd.round(2);
				line.Line_Total_FX = line_total_fx.round(2);
			}

			break;
		}
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_generate_pdf.deluge
================================================================================
void fn_generate_pdf(string p_quote_id)
{
	quoteIdLong = p_quote_id.toLong();
	for each quote in Quote_Request[ID == quoteIdLong]
	{
		quoteDate = zoho.currentdate.toString("yyyy-MM-dd");
		validUntil = zoho.currentdate.addDay(30).toString("yyyy-MM-dd");

		item_no = "1";
		item_desc = "";
		item_qty = "1";
		item_unit = "$0.00";
		item_line = "$0.00";
		item_ship = "4-6 weeks";

		item_no_joined = "";
		item_desc_joined = "";
		item_qty_joined = "";
		item_unit_joined = "";
		item_line_joined = "";
		lineCounter = 0;
		for each ln in quote.Quote_Lines
		{
			lineCounter = lineCounter + 1;
			rawQty = ifnull(ln.Qty, 0);
			rawUnit = ifnull(ln.Unit_Price, 0);
			rawLine = ifnull(ln.Line_Total_USD, 0);
			lnNo = ifnull(ln.Line_Number, lineCounter).toString();
			lnDesc = ifnull(ln.Description, "");
			lnQty = rawQty.round(0).toLong().toString();
			lnUnit = "$" + rawUnit.round(2).toString();
			lnLine = "$" + rawLine.round(2).toString();
			if(lineCounter > 1)
			{
				item_no_joined = item_no_joined + "\n";
				item_desc_joined = item_desc_joined + "\n";
				item_qty_joined = item_qty_joined + "\n";
				item_unit_joined = item_unit_joined + "\n";
				item_line_joined = item_line_joined + "\n";
			}
			item_no_joined = item_no_joined + lnNo;
			item_desc_joined = item_desc_joined + lnDesc;
			item_qty_joined = item_qty_joined + lnQty;
			item_unit_joined = item_unit_joined + lnUnit;
			item_line_joined = item_line_joined + lnLine;
		}
		if(lineCounter > 0)
		{
			item_no = item_no_joined;
			item_desc = item_desc_joined;
			item_qty = item_qty_joined;
			item_unit = item_unit_joined;
			item_line = item_line_joined;
		}

		subtotalAmt = 0.0;
		for each ln in quote.Quote_Lines
		{
			subtotalAmt = subtotalAmt + ifnull(ln.Line_Total_USD, 0);
		}
		subtotalAmt = subtotalAmt.round(2);

		blocked_marker = "";
		for each ln in quote.Quote_Lines
		{
			ln_desc = ifnull(ln.Description, "");
			if(ln_desc.contains("[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE"))
			{
				blocked_marker = "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE";
			}
			if(ln_desc.contains("[NO PRICE ON FILE"))
			{
				blocked_marker = "[NO PRICE ON FILE";
			}
		}
		if(blocked_marker != "")
		{
			info "fn_generate_pdf ABORT: loud-fail pricing marker " + blocked_marker + " present on quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number, "") + "' - Writer merge / Zoho Sign request NOT sent. Fix pricing/FX rates and resave.";
			return;
		}

		wireAmt = 25.00;
		shippingAmt = 25.00;
		taxAmt = 0.00;
		otherAmt = 0.00;
		fxAmt = 0.00;

		fxMode = ifnull(quote.FX_Charge_Mode, "non_usd_only");
		currency = ifnull(quote.Currency, "USD");

		if(fxMode == "always")
		{
			fxAmt = (subtotalAmt * 0.015).round(2);
		}
		else if(fxMode == "non_usd_only" && currency != "USD")
		{
			fxAmt = (subtotalAmt * 0.015).round(2);
		}
		else if(fxMode == "usd_only" && currency == "USD")
		{
			fxAmt = (subtotalAmt * 0.015).round(2);
		}

		totalAmt = (subtotalAmt + wireAmt + shippingAmt + taxAmt + fxAmt + otherAmt).round(2);

		qNum = ifnull(quote.Quote_Number, "");
		cCompany = ifnull(quote.Customer_Company, "");
		cName = ifnull(quote.Customer_Name, "");
		cEmail = ifnull(quote.Customer_Email, "");
		cPhoneRaw = ifnull(quote.Customer_Phone, "");
		cPhone = "";
		if(cPhoneRaw != "")
		{
			cPhoneText = cPhoneRaw.toString();
			if(cPhoneText.contains("phone_number"))
			{
				phoneParts = cPhoneText.toList("phone_number=");
				if(phoneParts.size() > 1)
				{
					cPhone = phoneParts.get(1).toString();
					cPhone = cPhone.replaceAll("[}\\],].*", "");
					cPhone = cPhone.replaceAll("\"", "").trim();
				}
			}
			else if(cPhoneText.contains("number"))
			{
				phoneParts = cPhoneText.toList("number=");
				if(phoneParts.size() > 1)
				{
					cPhone = phoneParts.get(1).toString();
					cPhone = cPhone.replaceAll("[}\\],].*", "");
					cPhone = cPhone.replaceAll("\"", "").trim();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				cPhone = cPhoneText.trim();
			}
		}
		rName = ifnull(quote.Recipient_Name, "Blake Allard");
		rEmail = ifnull(quote.Recipient_Email, "blake@bevco-tech.com");

		if(rEmail.trim() == "" || cEmail.trim() == "")
		{
			info "fn_generate_pdf ABORT: missing signer email. recipient_email='" + rEmail + "' customer_email='" + cEmail + "' quote=" + qNum;
			return;
		}

		mergeFields = Map();
		mergeFields.put("quote_number", qNum);
		mergeFields.put("quote_date", quoteDate);
		mergeFields.put("valid_until", validUntil);
		mergeFields.put("currency", currency);
		mergeFields.put("customer_company", cCompany);
		mergeFields.put("customer_name", cName);
		mergeFields.put("customer_email", cEmail);
		mergeFields.put("customer_phone", cPhone);
		mergeFields.put("recipient_name", rName);
		mergeFields.put("recipient_email", rEmail);
		mergeFields.put("company_phone", "+1 800-555-0100");
		mergeFields.put("payment_terms", "Net 30");
		mergeFields.put("lead_time", "4-6 weeks");
		mergeFields.put("shipping_terms", "FOB Origin");
		mergeFields.put("quote_validity", "30 days");
		mergeFields.put("fx_notes", "All prices in USD. No FX conversion applied.");
		mergeFields.put("additional_notes", "Pricing based on current distributor pricelist. Subject to change after expiration.");
		mergeFields.put("item_no", item_no);
		mergeFields.put("description", item_desc);
		mergeFields.put("qty", item_qty);
		mergeFields.put("unit_price", item_unit);
		mergeFields.put("line_total", item_line);
		mergeFields.put("ship_time_note", item_ship);
		mergeFields.put("subtotal", "$" + subtotalAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("tax", "$" + taxAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("shipping_handling", "$" + shippingAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("wire_charge", "$" + wireAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("fx_charge", "$" + fxAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("other", "$" + otherAmt.toString());
		mergeFields.put("total_revenue", "$" + totalAmt.toString());

		mergeDataList = List();
		mergeDataList.add(mergeFields);
		mergeData = Map();
		mergeData.put("data", mergeDataList);
		mergeJSON = mergeData.toString();

		signer1 = Map();
		signer1.put("recipient_1", rEmail);
		signer1.put("recipient_name", rName);
		signer1.put("action_type", "sign");
		signer1.put("private_notes", "Internal Bevco signature - review and authorize the quote before sending to customer.");
		signer2 = Map();
		signer2.put("recipient_2", cEmail);
		signer2.put("recipient_name", cName);
		signer2.put("action_type", "sign");
		signer2.put("private_notes", "Please review and sign the attached Kinetic Bridge quotation. Contact blake@bevco-tech.com with any questions.");
		signerJSON = "[" + signer1.toString() + "," + signer2.toString() + "]";

		params = Map();
		params.put("filename", "Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + qNum);
		params.put("service_name", "zohosign");
		params.put("sign_in_order", true);
		params.put("set_expire", 30);
		params.put("reminder_period", 3);
		params.put("message", "Please review and sign the attached Kinetic Bridge quotation. Contact blake@bevco-tech.com with any questions.");
		params.put("merge_data", mergeJSON);
		params.put("signer_data", signerJSON);

		response = invokeurl
		[
			url: "https://writer.zoho.com/writer/api/v1/documents/lg6haa9fe623ad500461ea6708547e49c8a4a/merge/sign"
			type: POST
			parameters: params
			connection: "zoho_writter"
		];

		info "fn_generate_pdf: " + response.toString();

		responseRecords = response.get("records");
		if(!isnull(responseRecords) && responseRecords.size() > 0)
		{
			docRecord = responseRecords.get(0);
			signReqId = ifnull(docRecord.get("sign_request_id"), "");
			if(signReqId != "")
			{
				for each rec in Quote_Request[ID == quoteIdLong]
				{
					rec.Sign_Request_ID = signReqId;
					break;
				}

				signUrl = ifnull(docRecord.get("download_link"), "");
				if(signUrl == "")
				{
					signUrl = "https://sign.zoho.com/signforms/" + signReqId;
				}
				noteTitle = "Quote Sent for Signature — " + qNum;
				noteContent = "Quote Number: " + qNum + "\n";
				noteContent = noteContent + "Date Sent: " + quoteDate + "\n";
				noteContent = noteContent + "Sign Request ID: " + signReqId + "\n";
				noteContent = noteContent + "Document Link: " + signUrl + "\n";
				noteContent = noteContent + "Signers: " + rName + " (internal) → " + cName + " (customer)";

				dealId = ifnull(quote.CRM_Deal_ID, "").trim();
				if(dealId == "")
				{
					dealName = cCompany + " — " + qNum;
					dealSearch = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Deals", "Deal_Name:equals:" + dealName);
					if(!isnull(dealSearch) && dealSearch.size() > 0)
					{
						dealId = dealSearch.get(0).get("id").toString();
					}
				}
				if(dealId != "")
				{
					dealNote = Map();
					dealNote.put("Note_Title", noteTitle);
					dealNote.put("Note_Content", noteContent);
					dealNote.put("Parent_Id", dealId);
					dealNote.put("se_module", "Deals");
					zoho.crm.createRecord("Notes", dealNote);
				}

				contactId = ifnull(quote.CRM_Contact_ID, "").trim();
				if(contactId == "" && cEmail != "")
				{
					contactSearch = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Contacts", "Email:equals:" + cEmail);
					if(!isnull(contactSearch) && contactSearch.size() > 0)
					{
						contactId = contactSearch.get(0).get("id").toString();
					}
				}
				if(contactId != "")
				{
					contactNote = Map();
					contactNote.put("Note_Title", noteTitle);
					contactNote.put("Note_Content", noteContent);
					contactNote.put("Parent_Id", contactId);
					contactNote.put("se_module", "Contacts");
					zoho.crm.createRecord("Notes", contactNote);
				}

				if(dealId != "")
				{
					pdfUrl = ifnull(docRecord.get("download_link"), "");
					if(pdfUrl == "")
					{
						info "fn_generate_pdf: no download_link in merge response for quote '" + qNum + "' - Deal PDF attachment SKIPPED.";
					}
					else
					{
						pdfFile = invokeurl
						[
							url :pdfUrl
							type :GET
							connection:"zoho_writter"
						];
						if(pdfFile == null)
						{
							info "fn_generate_pdf: PDF download failed from " + pdfUrl + " - Deal attachment SKIPPED for quote '" + qNum + "'.";
						}
						else
						{
							pdfFile.setParamName("file");
							pdfFile.setFileName("Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + qNum + ".pdf");
							attachResp = zoho.crm.attachFile("Deals", dealId.toLong(), pdfFile);
							info "fn_generate_pdf: Deal " + dealId + " PDF attach response: " + ifnull(attachResp, "null").toString();
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					info "fn_generate_pdf: no CRM_Deal_ID on quote '" + qNum + "' - Deal PDF attachment SKIPPED.";
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_get_discount.deluge
================================================================================
// fn_get_discount — updated 2026-06-30
// Signature matches both call sites (fn_calc_quote_lines, fn_sync_to_sheet):
// (customer_type, qty, is_software). Product_Type is derived internally from
// is_software so callers don't need to know about the Price_Rules schema.
//
// PREREQUISITE: Creator Price_Rules form must have these fields:
//   Customer_Type (Single Line)   — "Distributor", "Partner" ("End Customer" has
//                                    no rows on purpose -> falls through to 0%)
//   Product_Type  (Single Line)   — "Hardware" or "Software"
//   Qty_Min       (Number / Integer)
//   Qty_Max       (Number / Integer)
//   Discount_Pct  (Decimal)
//
// Per the July 2026 "Distrib discount" pricelist tab, Software discount is FLAT
// (not qty-tiered) — represent that as a single Software row per Customer_Type
// with Qty_Min=1, Qty_Max=999999. Hardware rows use these qty bands:
//   Partner:      1-9:20%  10-99:15%  100-249:12%  250-499:9%  500-999:7%  1000-2499:5%  2500-25000:3.5%
//   Distributor:  1-9:10%  10-99:7.5% 100-249:6%   250-499:4.5% 500-999:3.5% (no rows above 999 — 0%)
//   Software:     Partner 33% flat, Distributor 16.5% flat
//
// Returns discount as a fraction (e.g. 10% = 0.10).
// Non-discountable items (Items.Discountable = "N") must be handled by the caller,
// which should pass 0 directly and skip calling this function.

decimal fn_get_discount(string p_customer_type, decimal p_qty, boolean p_is_software)
{
	discountPct = 0.0;
	qtyLong = p_qty.round(0).toLong();
	productType = p_is_software ? "Software" : "Hardware";

	for each rule in Price_Rules[Customer_Type == p_customer_type && Product_Type == productType && Qty_Min <= qtyLong && Qty_Max >= qtyLong]
	{
		rawPct = ifnull(rule.Discount_Pct, 0.0);
		discountPct = rawPct / 100.0;
		break;
	}

	return discountPct;
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_get_kit_components.deluge
================================================================================
map fn_get_kit_components(string p_kit_key, int p_series_cells)
{
	result = Map();
	notices = List();
	lines = List();
	err = "";
	kit_key_local = ifnull(p_kit_key,"").toString().trim();
	if(kit_key_local == "")
	{
		err = "No kit selected.";
	}
	row_count = 0;
	mcu_qty = 0;
	cmu_qty = 0;
	qty_map = Map();
	if(err == "")
	{
		for each  comp in Kit_Components[Kit_Key == kit_key_local]
		{
			row_count = row_count + 1;
			rule = ifnull(comp.Qty_Rule,"").toString();
			if(rule == "fixed")
			{
				qv = ifnull(comp.Qty_Value,0);
				comp_id_text = comp.ID.toString();
				qty_map.put(comp_id_text,qv);
				req = ifnull(comp.Requirement,"").toString();
				if(req == "main")
				{
					mcu_qty = qv;
				}
			}
			else if(rule == "cmu_from_series_cells")
			{
				channels = ifnull(comp.Channels_Per_CMU,0);
				if(p_series_cells <= 0)
				{
					err = "Series Cell Count is required for this kit. Enter the series cell count and try again.";
					break;
				}
				if(p_series_cells < 4)
				{
					err = "Series Cell Count " + p_series_cells + " is below the 4-cell minimum.";
					break;
				}
				if(p_series_cells > 384)
				{
					err = "Series Cell Count " + p_series_cells + " is above the 384-cell maximum.";
					break;
				}
				if(channels <= 0)
				{
					err = "Kit data error: Channels Per CMU missing for SKU " + comp.Component_SKU + ".";
					break;
				}
				channels_dec = channels * 1.0;
				ratio = p_series_cells / channels_dec;
				cmu_dec = ratio.ceil();
				cmu_qty = cmu_dec.toLong();
				if(cmu_qty > 30)
				{
					err = "Series Cell Count " + p_series_cells + " needs " + cmu_qty + " CMUs, above the 30-CMU vendor cap.";
					break;
				}
				comp_id_text = comp.ID.toString();
				qty_map.put(comp_id_text,cmu_qty);
			}
		}
	}
	if(err == "")
	{
		if(row_count == 0)
		{
			err = "Unknown kit key: " + kit_key_local;
		}
	}
	if(err == "")
	{
		for each  comp in Kit_Components[Kit_Key == kit_key_local]
		{
			rule = ifnull(comp.Qty_Rule,"").toString();
			qty = 0;
			if(rule == "fixed")
			{
				comp_id_text = comp.ID.toString();
				qty_raw = qty_map.get(comp_id_text);
				qty = ifnull(qty_raw,0);
			}
			else if(rule == "cmu_from_series_cells")
			{
				qty = cmu_qty;
			}
			else if(rule == "match_mcu_qty")
			{
				if(mcu_qty <= 0)
				{
					err = "Kit data error: no main row to match for SKU " + comp.Component_SKU + ".";
					break;
				}
				qty = mcu_qty;
			}
			else if(rule == "match_cmu_qty")
			{
				if(cmu_qty <= 0)
				{
					err = "Kit data error: no CMU row to match for SKU " + comp.Component_SKU + ".";
					break;
				}
				qty = cmu_qty;
			}
			blocked = ifnull(comp.Is_Blocked,"N").toString();
			hold = ifnull(comp.Hold_Reason,"").toString().trim();
			req = ifnull(comp.Requirement,"").toString();
			if(blocked == "Y")
			{
				notices.add("OMITTED " + comp.Component_SKU + " (" + comp.Description + ") - not in RSP / unquotable. Do not substitute.");
			}
			else if(hold != "")
			{
				notices.add("HELD " + comp.Component_SKU + " (" + comp.Description + ") - open question " + hold + ". Add the confirmed SKU manually.");
			}
			else if(req == "manual")
			{
			}
			else
			{
				warn = ifnull(comp.Warning_Text,"").toString().trim();
				conf = ifnull(comp.Confidence,"").toString();
				if(conf == "pending_datasheet")
				{
					if(warn == "")
					{
						warn = "Quantity math unconfirmed - pending datasheet";
					}
					else
					{
						warn = warn + " | quantity math unconfirmed";
					}
				}
				line = Map();
				line.put("Part_Number",comp.Component_SKU);
				line.put("Qty",qty);
				line.put("Kit_Warning",warn);
				lines.add(line);
			}
		}
	}
	if(err != "")
	{
		lines = List();
	}
	result.put("error",err);
	result.put("notices",notices);
	result.put("lines",lines);
	return result;
}

# FILE: functions/fn_get_next_number.deluge
================================================================================
string fn_get_next_number(string p_type_code)
{
	found = false;
	doc_num = "";
	for each  rec in Document_Types[Type_Code == p_type_code]
	{
		found = true;
		next_seq = ifnull(rec.Last_Sequence,0) + 1;
		prefix = ifnull(rec.Prefix1,"");
		base = "0000" + next_seq.toString();
		padded = base.subString(base.length() - 4,base.length());
		doc_num = prefix + padded;
		rec.Last_Sequence=next_seq;
		insert into Document_Number_Log
		[
			Added_User=zoho.loginuser
			Document_Number=doc_num
			Type_Code=p_type_code
			Issued_At=zoho.currenttime
			Issued_By=zoho.loginuser
			Status="Active"
		]
		break;
	}
	if(found == false)
	{
		return "ERROR:UNKNOWN_TYPE";
	}
	return doc_num;
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_get_tier_price.deluge
================================================================================
// Tier_Scheme-aware line pricing. Full rationale/notes in
// docs/TIER_SCHEME_LINE_ITEM_FUNCTIONALITY_AUDIT.md and
// docs/TIER_SCHEME_CREATOR_DEPLOYMENT_RUNBOOK.md.
// Returns -1 (never a real price) if no valid price tier is found.
float fn_get_tier_price(string p_part_number, int p_qty)
{
	tier_price = -1;
	for each  item in Item_Master[Part_Number == p_part_number]
	{
		tiers = list();
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T1,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T2,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T3,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T4,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T5,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T6,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T7,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T8,null));
		tiers.add(ifnull(item.Price_T9,null));

		// Blank/unknown Tier_Scheme defaults to hardware (not license).
		tier_scheme = ifnull(item.Tier_Scheme,"").toString().trim().toLowerCase();
		if(tier_scheme != "hardware" && tier_scheme != "license")
		{
			tier_scheme = "hardware";
		}

		tier_index = 0;
		if(tier_scheme == "license")
		{
			if(p_qty <= 1) { tier_index = 0; }
			else if(p_qty <= 2) { tier_index = 1; }
			else if(p_qty <= 4) { tier_index = 2; }
			else if(p_qty <= 9) { tier_index = 3; }
			else if(p_qty <= 24) { tier_index = 4; }
			else { tier_index = 5; }
		}
		else
		{
			if(p_qty <= 19) { tier_index = 0; }
			else if(p_qty <= 99) { tier_index = 1; }
			else if(p_qty <= 259) { tier_index = 2; }
			else if(p_qty <= 499) { tier_index = 3; }
			else if(p_qty <= 999) { tier_index = 4; }
			else if(p_qty <= 2499) { tier_index = 5; }
			else if(p_qty <= 4999) { tier_index = 6; }
			else if(p_qty <= 9999) { tier_index = 7; }
			else { tier_index = 8; }
		}

		// Walk down to the highest published tier at or below the requested qty
		// tier. Unchanged from prior behavior; works identically for both schemes.
		i = tier_index;
		while(i >= 0 && tiers.get(i) == null)
		{
			i = i - 1;
		}
		if(i >= 0)
		{
			tier_price = tiers.get(i);
		}
		// else: leave tier_price at the -1 "no valid price found" sentinel.
		break;
	}
	return tier_price;
}

# FILE: functions/fn_refresh_fx_rates.deluge
================================================================================
void fn_refresh_fx_rates()
{
	response = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/USD"
		type :GET
	];
	result_map = response.toMap();
	if(result_map.get("result") == "success")
	{
		rates = result_map.get("rates").toMap();
		currencies = list();
		currencies.add("EUR");
		currencies.add("GBP");
		currencies.add("CAD");
		currencies.add("AUD");
		currencies.add("JPY");
		currencies.add("CNY");
		currencies.add("KRW");
		currencies.add("DKK");
		for each  curr in currencies
		{
			rate = rates.get(curr).toDecimal();
			found = false;
			for each  rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == curr]
			{
				rec.Rate=rate;
				rec.Cached_At=zoho.currenttime;
				found = true;
				break;
			}
			if(found == false)
			{
				insert into FX_Rates_Cache
				[
					Currency=curr
					Rate=rate
					Cached_At=zoho.currenttime
					Added_User=zoho.loginuser
				]
			}
		}
	}
}

void fn_send_to_sign(string p_quote_id)
{
	template_id = "504457000000201063";
	for each  quote in Quote_Request[ID == p_quote_id.toLong()]
	{
		customer_name = ifnull(quote.Customer_Name,"");
		customer_email = ifnull(quote.Customer_Email,"");
		quote_number = ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"");
		actions_json = "[{\"action_id\":\"504457000000201075\",\"recipient_name\":\"Blake Allard\",\"recipient_email\":\"blake@bevco-tech.com\"},{\"action_id\":\"504457000000201077\",\"recipient_name\":\"" + customer_name + "\",\"recipient_email\":\"" + customer_email + "\"}]";
		data_json = "{\"templates\":{\"field_data\":{\"field_text_data\":{},\"field_boolean_data\":{},\"field_date_data\":{}},\"actions\":" + actions_json + ",\"notes\":\"Please review and sign quote " + quote_number + "\"}}";
		send_params = Map();
		send_params.put("data",data_json);
		response = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sign.zoho.com/api/v1/templates/" + template_id + "/createsendrequest"
			type :POST
			parameters:send_params
			connection:"zoho_sign_connection"
		];
		for each  rec in Quote_Request[ID == p_quote_id.toLong()]
		{
			rec.Notes=response.toString();
			break;
		}
		break;
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_sync_to_crm.deluge
================================================================================
void fn_sync_to_crm(string p_quote_id)
{
	for each  quote in Quote_Request[ID == p_quote_id.toLong()]
	{
		status = ifnull(quote.Status,"Draft");
		total_revenue = 0.0;
		for each  line in quote.Quote_Lines
		{
			total_revenue = total_revenue + ifnull(line.Line_Total_USD,0);
		}
		contact_id = ifnull(quote.CRM_Contact_ID,"").trim();
		pending_lead_id = ifnull(quote.CRM_Lead_ID,"").trim();
		existing_deal_id = ifnull(quote.CRM_Deal_ID,"").trim();
		if(contact_id == "" && pending_lead_id != "")
		{
			lead_probe = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Leads",pending_lead_id.toLong());
			if(lead_probe == null)
			{
				info "fn_sync_to_crm: CRM_Lead_ID " + pending_lead_id + " not found (already converted or deleted) - falling through to email-based Contact resolution for quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "'.";
			}
			else
			{
				convert_body = Map();
				convert_entry = Map();
				convert_entry.put("overwrite",false);
				convert_entry.put("notify_lead_owner",false);
				convert_entry.put("notify_new_entity_owner",false);
				if(existing_deal_id == "")
				{
					deal_seed = Map();
					deal_name_seed = ifnull(quote.Customer_Company,"").trim();
					if(deal_name_seed == "")
					{
						deal_name_seed = ifnull(quote.Customer_Name,"Unknown").trim();
					}
					quote_no_seed = ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"").toString().trim();
					if(quote_no_seed != "")
					{
						deal_name_seed = deal_name_seed + " - " + quote_no_seed;
					}
					deal_seed.put("Deal_Name",deal_name_seed);
					deal_seed.put("Stage","Proposal/Price Quote");
					deal_seed.put("Amount",total_revenue.round(2));
					deal_seed.put("Closing_Date",zoho.currentdate.addDay(30).toString("yyyy-MM-dd"));
					convert_entry.put("Deals",deal_seed);
				}
				convert_list = List();
				convert_list.add(convert_entry);
				convert_body.put("data",convert_list);
				convert_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Leads/" + pending_lead_id + "/actions/convert"
					type :POST
					parameters:convert_body.toString()
					connection:"zoho_crm_connection"
				];
				converted_contact = "";
				converted_account = "";
				converted_deal = "";
				if(convert_response != null)
				{
					convert_map = convert_response.toMap();
					if(convert_map != null)
					{
						convert_rows = convert_map.getJSON("data");
						if(convert_rows != null)
						{
							for each  convert_row in convert_rows
							{
								convert_row_map = convert_row.toMap();
								if(convert_row_map != null)
								{
									converted_contact = ifnull(convert_row_map.get("Contacts"),"").toString();
									converted_account = ifnull(convert_row_map.get("Accounts"),"").toString();
									converted_deal = ifnull(convert_row_map.get("Deals"),"").toString();
								}
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if(converted_contact == "" || converted_contact == "null")
				{
					info "fn_sync_to_crm: Lead convert FAILED for Lead " + pending_lead_id + " on quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "' - response: " + ifnull(convert_response,"null").toString() + ". Quote left unlinked; fix in CRM and resave.";
				}
				else
				{
					contact_id = converted_contact;
					quote.CRM_Contact_ID = converted_contact;
					if(converted_account != "" && converted_account != "null")
					{
						quote.CRM_Account_ID = converted_account;
					}
					if(existing_deal_id == "")
					{
						if(converted_deal != "" && converted_deal != "null")
						{
							quote.CRM_Deal_ID = converted_deal;
						}
					}
					info "fn_sync_to_crm: Lead " + pending_lead_id + " CONVERTED for quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "' - Contact " + converted_contact + ", Account " + converted_account + ", Deal " + converted_deal + ".";
				}
			}
		}
		if(contact_id == "")
		{
			contact_email = ifnull(quote.Customer_Email,"");
			if(contact_email != "")
			{
				contact_search = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Contacts","Email:equals:" + contact_email);
				if(contact_search != null)
				{
					if(contact_search.size() > 0)
					{
						contact_id = contact_search.get(0).get("id").toString();
					}
				}
				if(contact_id == "")
				{
					contact_data = Map();
					contact_data.put("Last_Name",ifnull(quote.Customer_Name,"Unknown"));
					contact_data.put("Email",contact_email);
					contact_data.put("Account_Name",ifnull(quote.Customer_Company,""));
					new_contact = zoho.crm.createRecord("Contacts",contact_data);
					if(new_contact != null)
					{
						contact_id = ifnull(new_contact.get("id"),"").toString();
					}
				}
				if(contact_id != "")
				{
					quote.CRM_Contact_ID = contact_id;
				}
			}
		}
		if(contact_id != "")
		{
			lead_id_text = "";
			persisted_lead_id = ifnull(quote.CRM_Lead_ID,"").trim();
			if(persisted_lead_id != "")
			{
				lead_id_text = persisted_lead_id;
			}
			quote_email = ifnull(quote.Customer_Email,"").trim();
			run_lookup = false;
			if(lead_id_text == "")
			{
				if(quote_email != "")
				{
					run_lookup = true;
				}
			}
			if(run_lookup)
			{
				lead_search_url = "https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Leads/search?converted=true&email=" + encodeUrl(quote_email);
				lead_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :lead_search_url
					type :GET
					connection:"zoho_crm_connection"
				];
				lead_rows = List();
				if(lead_response != null)
				{
					lead_result_map = lead_response.toMap();
					if(lead_result_map != null)
					{
						lead_rows_raw = lead_result_map.getJSON("data");
						if(lead_rows_raw != null)
						{
							lead_rows.addAll(lead_rows_raw);
						}
					}
				}
				for each  lead_row in lead_rows
				{
					if(lead_id_text == "")
					{
						row_text = lead_row.toString();
						if(row_text.contains(contact_id))
						{
							lead_row_map = row_text.toMap();
							if(lead_row_map != null)
							{
								lead_id_text = ifnull(lead_row_map.get("id"),"").toString();
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if(lead_id_text != "")
			{
				quote.CRM_Lead_ID = lead_id_text;
			}
			already_stamped = ifnull(quote.Lifecycle_Stamped,false);
			quote_number_text = ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"").toString();
			run_stamp = false;
			if(already_stamped == false)
			{
				if(quote_number_text != "")
				{
					run_stamp = true;
				}
			}
			if(run_stamp)
			{
				contact_rec = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Contacts",contact_id.toLong());
				existing_first_quote = "";
				if(contact_rec != null)
				{
					existing_first_quote = ifnull(contact_rec.get("First_Quote_Number"),"").toString();
				}
				if(existing_first_quote == "")
				{
					contact_stamp = Map();
					contact_stamp.put("First_Quote_Number",quote_number_text);
					first_quote_at = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss");
					contact_stamp.put("First_Quote_Created_At",first_quote_at + "-07:00");
					contact_stamp.put("Lifecycle_Stage","Quoted");
					zoho.crm.updateRecord("Contacts",contact_id.toLong(),contact_stamp);
				}
				quote.Lifecycle_Stamped = true;
			}
		}
		deal_id_text = ifnull(quote.CRM_Deal_ID,"").trim();
		if(deal_id_text == "")
		{
			info "fn_sync_to_crm: no CRM_Deal_ID on quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "' - Deal sync SKIPPED. Select an existing Deal or use Create New Opportunity in the widget (no silent Deal creation).";
		}
		else
		{
			deal_rec = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",deal_id_text.toLong());
			if(deal_rec == null)
			{
				info "fn_sync_to_crm: CRM Deal " + deal_id_text + " not found by ID - Deal sync SKIPPED for quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "' (stale CRM_Deal_ID; reselect the Deal in the widget).";
			}
			else
			{
				stage_rank = Map();
				stage_rank.put("Qualification",1);
				stage_rank.put("Needs Analysis",2);
				stage_rank.put("Value Proposition",3);
				stage_rank.put("Id. Decision Makers",4);
				stage_rank.put("Proposal/Price Quote",5);
				stage_rank.put("Negotiation/Review",6);
				stage_rank.put("Closed Won",7);
				closed_stages = List();
				closed_stages.add("Closed Won");
				closed_stages.add("Closed Lost");
				closed_stages.add("Closed Lost to Competition");
				target_stage = "";
				if(status == "Draft") { target_stage = "Proposal/Price Quote"; }
				if(status == "In Review") { target_stage = "Proposal/Price Quote"; }
				if(status == "Send for Signature") { target_stage = "Negotiation/Review"; }
				if(status == "Awaiting Signatures") { target_stage = "Negotiation/Review"; }
				if(status == "Signed") { target_stage = "Closed Won"; }
				current_stage = ifnull(deal_rec.get("Stage"),"").toString();
				deal_update = Map();
				deal_update.put("Amount",total_revenue.round(2));
				if(target_stage != "")
				{
					if(closed_stages.contains(current_stage))
					{
						info "fn_sync_to_crm: Deal " + deal_id_text + " is terminal ('" + current_stage + "') - stage untouched (never reopen automatically).";
					}
					else
					{
						current_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(current_stage),0);
						target_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(target_stage),0);
						if(target_rank > current_rank)
						{
							deal_update.put("Stage",target_stage);
						}
					}
				}
				current_quote_fields_ready = false;
				if(current_quote_fields_ready)
				{
					if(status == "Cancelled")
					{
						deal_update.put("Current_Quote_Number","");
						deal_update.put("Current_Quote_Link","");
						deal_update.put("Current_Quote_Revision","");
						deal_update.put("QTS_Quote_Record_ID","");
					}
					else
					{
						deal_update.put("Current_Quote_Number",ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,""));
						deal_update.put("QTS_Quote_Record_ID",p_quote_id);
					}
				}
				missing_products = "";
				assoc_rows = List();
				unique_parts = List();
				seen_parts = Map();
				for each  line in quote.Quote_Lines
				{
					part_no = ifnull(line.Part_Number,"").toString().trim();
					if(part_no != "")
					{
						if(ifnull(seen_parts.get(part_no),"") == "")
						{
							unique_parts.add(part_no);
							seen_parts.put(part_no,"1");
						}
					}
				}
				product_by_code = Map();
				if(unique_parts.size() > 0)
				{
					// CRM search criteria caps ~10 OR conditions — batch Product lookups.
					product_criteria = "";
					batch_count = 0;
					for each  part_no in unique_parts
					{
						piece = "(Product_Code:equals:" + part_no + ")";
						if(product_criteria == "")
						{
							product_criteria = piece;
						}
						else
						{
							product_criteria = product_criteria + "or" + piece;
						}
						batch_count = batch_count + 1;
						if(batch_count >= 8)
						{
							product_matches = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Products",product_criteria);
							if(product_matches != null)
							{
								for each  prod in product_matches
								{
									code = ifnull(prod.get("Product_Code"),"").toString().trim();
									pid = ifnull(prod.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
									if(code != "" && pid != "")
									{
										product_by_code.put(code,pid);
									}
								}
							}
							product_criteria = "";
							batch_count = 0;
						}
					}
					if(product_criteria != "")
					{
						product_matches = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Products",product_criteria);
						if(product_matches != null)
						{
							for each  prod in product_matches
							{
								code = ifnull(prod.get("Product_Code"),"").toString().trim();
								pid = ifnull(prod.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
								if(code != "" && pid != "")
								{
									product_by_code.put(code,pid);
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				for each  line in quote.Quote_Lines
				{
					part_no = ifnull(line.Part_Number,"").toString().trim();
					if(part_no != "")
					{
						product_id = ifnull(product_by_code.get(part_no),"").toString().trim();
						if(product_id == "")
						{
							if(missing_products != "")
							{
								missing_products = missing_products + ", ";
							}
							missing_products = missing_products + part_no;
						}
						else
						{
							assoc_row = Map();
							product_ref = Map();
							product_ref.put("id",product_id);
							assoc_row.put("Product",product_ref);
							assoc_row.put("Quantity",ifnull(line.Qty,0));
							assoc_row.put("List_Price",ifnull(line.Unit_Price,0));
							assoc_row.put("Total",ifnull(line.Line_Total_USD,0));
							assoc_rows.add(assoc_row);
						}
					}
				}
				// Never wipe existing Deal products when lookup returns zero rows but lines exist.
				if(assoc_rows.size() > 0)
				{
					deal_update.put("Associated_Products",assoc_rows);
				}
				else if(unique_parts.size() > 0)
				{
					info "fn_sync_to_crm: refusing to write empty Associated_Products for Deal " + deal_id_text + " — product lookup returned 0 rows for " + unique_parts.size() + " part(s). Existing Deal products left unchanged.";
				}
				update_resp = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",deal_id_text.toLong(),deal_update);
				if(missing_products != "")
				{
					info "fn_sync_to_crm: CRM Products missing for part(s) " + missing_products + " - those lines are NOT on the Deal Associated Products list. Add them to CRM Products (Product_Code = Part_Number) and resave.";
				}
				info "fn_sync_to_crm: Deal " + deal_id_text + " updated with " + assoc_rows.size() + " Associated Product row(s) for quote '" + ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"") + "': " + ifnull(update_resp,"null").toString();
				info "fn_sync_to_crm: CRM Quotes skipped on sync — email path owns Quotes module.";
			}
		}
		break;
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: functions/fn_sync_to_sheet.deluge
================================================================================
void fn_sync_to_sheet(string p_quote_id)
{
	resource_id = "arkte96b9da3a82a542ec8ac5ba1b713ba7c4";
	quote_id_long = p_quote_id.toLong();
	for each  quote in Quote_Request[ID == quote_id_long]
	{
		sync_status = ifnull(quote.Status,"Draft");
		run_sync = false;
		if(sync_status == "Send for Signature")
		{
			run_sync = true;
		}
		if(sync_status == "Signed")
		{
			run_sync = true;
		}
		if(run_sync == false)
		{
			break;
		}
		quote_num = ifnull(quote.Quote_Number,"");
		// Loud-fail FX policy (matches fn_calc_quote_lines): a missing/zero rate
		// must never silently become 1 - carry 0 and mark the sheet row instead.
		eur_usd = ifnull(quote.EUR_USD_Rate,0);
		customer_type = ifnull(quote.Customer_Type,"End Customer");
		currency = ifnull(quote.Currency,"USD");
		fx_rate = 1.0;
		fx_rate_found = false;
		for each  rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == currency]
		{
			fx_rate = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
			fx_rate_found = true;
			break;
		}
		if(currency != "USD")
		{
			if(fx_rate_found == false)
			{
				fx_rate = 0.0;
			}
		}
		fx_alert = "";
		if(eur_usd == 0)
		{
			fx_alert = "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE - EUR_USD 0 - DO NOT USE MARGINS]";
		}
		if(currency != "USD")
		{
			if(fx_rate == 0)
			{
				fx_alert = fx_alert + "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE FOR " + currency + "]";
			}
		}
		// Quote_Request tab: find + delete existing rows, then insert.
		qr_fetch_params = Map();
		qr_fetch_params.put("method","worksheet.records.fetch");
		qr_fetch_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Request");
		qr_fetch_params.put("header_row",1);
		qr_fetch_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
			type :GET
			parameters:qr_fetch_params
			connection:"zoho_sheet"
		];
		qr_indices = "";
		qr_all = qr_fetch_resp.get("records");
		if(qr_all != null)
		{
			for each  qr_rec in qr_all
			{
				if(ifnull(qr_rec.get("Quote_Number"),"").toString() == quote_num)
				{
					if(qr_indices != "") { qr_indices = qr_indices + ","; }
					qr_indices = qr_indices + qr_rec.get("row_index").toString();
				}
			}
		}
		if(qr_indices != "")
		{
			qr_del_params = Map();
			qr_del_params.put("method","worksheet.records.delete");
			qr_del_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Request");
			qr_del_params.put("row_array","[" + qr_indices + "]");
			qr_del_params.put("delete_rows","true");
			invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
				type :POST
				parameters:qr_del_params
				connection:"zoho_sheet"
			]
		}
		qr_row = Map();
		qr_row.put("Quote_Number",quote_num);
		qr_row.put("Customer_Name",ifnull(quote.Customer_Company,""));
		qr_row.put("Customer_Type",customer_type);
		qr_row.put("Contact_Name",ifnull(quote.Customer_Name,""));
		qr_row.put("EUR_USD_Rate",eur_usd);
		qr_row.put("DKK_per_USD_Rate",fx_rate);
		qr_row.put("Markup_Rate_Pct",ifnull(quote.Markup_Rate_Pct,0));
		qr_row.put("Wire_Charge_USD",25);
		qr_row.put("Status",ifnull(quote.Status,"Draft"));
		qr_notes = ifnull(quote.Notes,"");
		if(fx_alert != "")
		{
			qr_notes = fx_alert + " " + qr_notes;
		}
		qr_row.put("Notes",qr_notes);
		qr_row.put("Created_By","Blake Allard");
		qr_row.put("Created_Date",zoho.currentdate.toString("M/d/yyyy"));
		qr_add_params = Map();
		qr_add_params.put("method","worksheet.records.add");
		qr_add_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Request");
		qr_add_params.put("header_row",1);
		qr_add_params.put("json_data","[" + qr_row.toString() + "]");
		invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
			type :POST
			parameters:qr_add_params
			connection:"zoho_sheet"
		]
		// Quote_Lines tab: find + delete existing rows, then insert.
		ql_fetch_params = Map();
		ql_fetch_params.put("method","worksheet.records.fetch");
		ql_fetch_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Lines");
		ql_fetch_params.put("header_row",1);
		ql_fetch_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
			type :GET
			parameters:ql_fetch_params
			connection:"zoho_sheet"
		];
		ql_indices = "";
		ql_all = ql_fetch_resp.get("records");
		if(ql_all != null)
		{
			for each  lr in ql_all
			{
				if(ifnull(lr.get("Quote_Number_Ref"),"").toString() == quote_num)
				{
					if(ql_indices != "") { ql_indices = ql_indices + ","; }
					ql_indices = ql_indices + lr.get("row_index").toString();
				}
			}
		}
		if(ql_indices != "")
		{
			ql_del_params = Map();
			ql_del_params.put("method","worksheet.records.delete");
			ql_del_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Lines");
			ql_del_params.put("row_array","[" + ql_indices + "]");
			ql_del_params.put("delete_rows","true");
			invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
				type :POST
				parameters:ql_del_params
				connection:"zoho_sheet"
			]
		}
		total_revenue = 0.0;
		total_cost = 0.0;
		ql_rows_json = "";
		for each  line in quote.Quote_Lines
		{
			qty = ifnull(line.Qty,0);
			part = ifnull(line.Part_Number,"");
			eur_price = thisapp.fn_get_tier_price(part,qty.toLong());
			// -1 sentinel means no valid price was found; same guard as
			// fn_calc_quote_lines.deluge. Description marker not re-added here;
			// fn_calc_quote_lines already set it and runs first in the save flow.
			price_found = (eur_price >= 0);
			if(!price_found)
			{
				eur_price = 0;
			}
			usd_cost_unit = eur_price * eur_usd;
			discount = 0.0;
			for each  item in Item_Master[Part_Number == part]
			{
				discountable_val = ifnull(item.Discountable,"N");
				is_discountable = false;
				if(discountable_val == "Y")
				{
					is_discountable = true;
				}
				category_val = ifnull(item.Category,"");
				is_software = false;
				if(category_val == "Software")
				{
					is_software = true;
				}
				if(is_discountable)
				{
					discount = thisapp.fn_get_discount(customer_type,qty.toLong(),is_software);
				}
				break;
			}
			line_cost_usd = (qty * usd_cost_unit * (1 - discount)).round(2);
			line_revenue_usd = ifnull(line.Line_Total_USD,0);
			gross_margin_usd = (line_revenue_usd - line_cost_usd).round(2);
			gross_margin_pct = 0.0;
			if(line_revenue_usd != 0)
			{
				gross_margin_pct = (gross_margin_usd / line_revenue_usd * 100).round(2);
			}
			ql_row = Map();
			ql_row.put("Quote_Number_Ref",quote_num);
			ql_row.put("Part_Number_Ref",part);
			ql_row.put("Product_Description",ifnull(line.Description,""));
			ql_row.put("Quantity",qty);
			ql_row.put("EUR_List_Price",eur_price);
			ql_row.put("Discount_Pct",discount);
			ql_row.put("Markup_Rate_Pct",ifnull(quote.Markup_Rate_Pct,0));
			ql_row.put("EUR_USD_Rate",eur_usd);
			ql_row.put("DKK_per_USD_Rate",fx_rate);
			ql_row.put("Unit_Cost_USD",usd_cost_unit.round(2));
			ql_row.put("Ship_Time_Note","");
			ql_row.put("Notes","");
			ql_row.put("EUR_After_Discount",(eur_price * (1 - discount)).round(2));
			ql_row.put("Line_Cost_USD",line_cost_usd);
			ql_row.put("Customer_Unit_Price_USD",ifnull(line.Unit_Price,0));
			ql_row.put("Customer_Unit_Price_DKK",ifnull(line.FX_Unit_Price,0));
			ql_row.put("Line_Revenue_USD",line_revenue_usd);
			ql_row.put("Gross_Margin_USD",gross_margin_usd);
			ql_row.put("Gross_Margin_Pct",gross_margin_pct);
			if(ql_rows_json != "")
			{
				ql_rows_json = ql_rows_json + ",";
			}
			ql_rows_json = ql_rows_json + ql_row.toString();
			total_revenue = total_revenue + line_revenue_usd;
			total_cost = total_cost + line_cost_usd;
		}
		if(ql_rows_json != "")
		{
			ql_add_params = Map();
			ql_add_params.put("method","worksheet.records.add");
			ql_add_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Lines");
			ql_add_params.put("header_row",1);
			ql_add_params.put("json_data","[" + ql_rows_json + "]");
			invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
				type :POST
				parameters:ql_add_params
				connection:"zoho_sheet"
			]
		}
		// Quote_Output tab: find + delete existing rows, then insert.
		wire_charge = 25;
		fx_charge = (total_revenue * 0.015).round(2);
		gross_margin = (total_revenue - total_cost).round(2);
		total_fees = (wire_charge + fx_charge).round(2);
		net_net = (gross_margin - total_fees).round(2);
		qo_fetch_params = Map();
		qo_fetch_params.put("method","worksheet.records.fetch");
		qo_fetch_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Output");
		qo_fetch_params.put("header_row",1);
		qo_fetch_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
			type :GET
			parameters:qo_fetch_params
			connection:"zoho_sheet"
		];
		qo_indices = "";
		qo_all = qo_fetch_resp.get("records");
		if(qo_all != null)
		{
			for each  qo_rec in qo_all
			{
				if(ifnull(qo_rec.get("Quote_Number_Ref"),"").toString() == quote_num)
				{
					if(qo_indices != "") { qo_indices = qo_indices + ","; }
					qo_indices = qo_indices + qo_rec.get("row_index").toString();
				}
			}
		}
		if(qo_indices != "")
		{
			qo_del_params = Map();
			qo_del_params.put("method","worksheet.records.delete");
			qo_del_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Output");
			qo_del_params.put("row_array","[" + qo_indices + "]");
			qo_del_params.put("delete_rows","true");
			invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
				type :POST
				parameters:qo_del_params
				connection:"zoho_sheet"
			]
		}
		qo_row = Map();
		qo_row.put("Quote_Number_Ref",quote_num);
		qo_row.put("Total_Revenue_USD",total_revenue.round(2));
		qo_row.put("Total_Cost_USD",total_cost.round(2));
		qo_row.put("Wire_Charge_USD",wire_charge);
		qo_row.put("Gross_Margin_USD",gross_margin);
		qo_row.put("FX_Charge_USD",fx_charge);
		qo_row.put("Total_Fees_USD",total_fees);
		qo_row.put("Net_Net_Profit_USD",net_net);
		qo_add_params = Map();
		qo_add_params.put("method","worksheet.records.add");
		qo_add_params.put("worksheet_name","Quote_Output");
		qo_add_params.put("header_row",1);
		qo_add_params.put("json_data","[" + qo_row.toString() + "]");
		invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://sheet.zoho.com/api/v2/" + resource_id
			type :POST
			parameters:qo_add_params
			connection:"zoho_sheet"
		]
		break;
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: deploy_ready/crm_bridge_actions/search_customers.creator.deluge
================================================================================
// Paste into workflow: CRM Bridge - search_customers (replace entire Deluge).
out = Map();
action_name = ifnull(input.Action_field,"").trim();
input.Request_Status = "error";
try
{
	if(action_name != "search_customers" && action_name != "")
	{
		out.put("error","This workflow only handles search_customers (got Action_field=" + action_name + ")");
	}
	else
	{
		query_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
		matches = List();
		seen_ids = List();
		if(query_text != "")
		{
			contact_criteria = "(Email:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Last_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(First_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")";
			has_space = query_text.contains(" ");
			if(has_space)
			{
				name_tokens = query_text.toList(" ");
				token_count = name_tokens.size();
				first_token = name_tokens.get(0).trim();
				last_index = token_count - 1;
				last_token = name_tokens.get(last_index).trim();
				if(first_token != "" && last_token != "")
				{
					contact_criteria = contact_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + "))";
					contact_criteria = contact_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + "))";
				}
			}
			found = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Contacts",contact_criteria);
			if(found != null)
			{
				for each  contact in found
				{
					contact_id = contact.get("id").toString();
					if(!seen_ids.contains(contact_id))
					{
						seen_ids.add(contact_id);
						row = Map();
						row.put("contact_id",contact_id);
						row.put("name",ifnull(contact.get("Full_Name"),""));
						row.put("email",ifnull(contact.get("Email"),""));
						row.put("phone",ifnull(contact.get("Phone"),""));
						account_ref = contact.get("Account_Name");
						if(account_ref != null)
						{
							row.put("account_id",ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString());
							row.put("account_name",ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),""));
						}
						matches.add(row);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		out.put("matches",matches);
		input.Request_Status = "done";
	}
}
catch (bridge_err)
{
	out.put("error","search_customers failed: " + bridge_err.toString());
	input.Request_Status = "error";
}
input.Result_jSON = out.toString();

// Paste into workflow: search_leads (replace entire Deluge).
out = Map();
action_name = ifnull(input.Action_field,"").trim();
input.Request_Status = "error";
try
{
	if(action_name != "search_leads" && action_name != "")
	{
		out.put("error","This workflow only handles search_leads (got Action_field=" + action_name + ")");
	}
	else
	{
		query_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
		lead_matches = List();
		seen_lead_ids = List();
		if(query_text != "")
		{
			lead_criteria = "(Email:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Last_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(First_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Company:starts_with:" + query_text + ")";
			has_space = query_text.contains(" ");
			if(has_space)
			{
				name_tokens = query_text.toList(" ");
				token_count = name_tokens.size();
				first_token = name_tokens.get(0).trim();
				last_index = token_count - 1;
				last_token = name_tokens.get(last_index).trim();
				if(first_token != "" && last_token != "")
				{
					lead_criteria = lead_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + "))";
					lead_criteria = lead_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + "))";
				}
			}
			found_leads = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Leads",lead_criteria);
			if(found_leads != null)
			{
				for each  lead in found_leads
				{
					lead_id_val = ifnull(lead.get("id"),"").toString();
					if(lead_id_val != "")
					{
						if(!seen_lead_ids.contains(lead_id_val))
						{
							is_converted = ifnull(lead.get("$converted"),false);
							if(is_converted == false)
							{
								seen_lead_ids.add(lead_id_val);
								row = Map();
								row.put("lead_id",lead_id_val);
								row.put("name",ifnull(lead.get("Full_Name"),""));
								row.put("email",ifnull(lead.get("Email"),""));
								row.put("phone",ifnull(lead.get("Phone"),""));
								row.put("company",ifnull(lead.get("Company"),""));
								lead_matches.add(row);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		out.put("lead_matches",lead_matches);
		input.Request_Status = "done";
	}
}
catch (bridge_err)
{
	out.put("error","search_leads failed: " + bridge_err.toString());
	input.Request_Status = "error";
}
input.Result_jSON = out.toString();

	if(quote_id_text == "")

				break;
			}
			out.put("status","synced");
			out.put("quote_id",quote_id_text);
			out.put("crm_deal_id",deal_id_out);
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		catch (sync_err)

			out.put("error","sync_quote_to_crm failed: " + sync_err.toString());
