// Creator subform On User Input context: the triggering Quote_Lines row is
// exposed as the pre-defined `row` variable; `input.` resolves parent
// Quote_Request fields only. Same split proven by the deployed Part_Select
// workflow (deploy_ready/on_user_input_quote_lines_part_select.creator.deluge).
qty_set = false;
if(row.Qty != null)
{
	if(row.Qty > 0)
	{
		qty_set = true;
	}
}

if(qty_set)
{
	part = "";
	desc = ifnull(row.Description,"");
	if(row.Part_Select != null)
	{
		part = ifnull(row.Part_Select.Part_Number,"").toString();
	}

	part_is_blank = false;
	if(part == "")
	{
		part_is_blank = true;
	}
	if(part == "null")
	{
		part_is_blank = true;
	}

	if(part_is_blank)
	{
		part = ifnull(row.Part_Number,"").toString();
	}

	part_is_usable = true;
	if(part == "")
	{
		part_is_usable = false;
	}
	if(part == "null")
	{
		part_is_usable = false;
	}

	if(part_is_usable)
	{
		calc = thisapp.fn_calc_line_price_draft(
			part,
			row.Qty,
			desc,
			ifnull(input.Customer_Type,"End Customer"),
			ifnull(input.Currency,"USD"),
			ifnull(input.Markup_Rate_Pct,0),
			ifnull(input.FX_Charge_Mode,"non_usd_only")
		);
		row.Unit_Price = calc.get("unit_price");
		row.FX_Unit_Price = calc.get("fx_unit_price");
		row.Line_Total_USD = calc.get("line_total_usd");
		row.Line_Total_FX = calc.get("line_total_fx");
		row.Description = calc.get("description");
		// row.Discount = calc.get("discount");
		// row.Discountable = calc.get("discountable");
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: workflows/crm_bridge_on_create.deluge
================================================================================

out = Map();
action_name = ifnull(input.Action_field,"");
input.Request_Status = "error";

if(action_name == "search_customers")
{
	query_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	matches = List();
	seen_ids = List();
	if(query_text != "")
	{
		contact_criteria = "(Email:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Last_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(First_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")";
		has_space = query_text.contains(" ");
		if(has_space)
		{
			name_tokens = query_text.toList(" ");
			token_count = name_tokens.size();
			first_token = name_tokens.get(0).trim();
			last_index = token_count - 1;
			last_token = name_tokens.get(last_index).trim();
			if(first_token != "" && last_token != "")
			{
				contact_criteria = contact_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + "))";
				contact_criteria = contact_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + "))";
			}
		}
		found = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Contacts",contact_criteria);
		if(found != null)
		{
			for each  contact in found
			{
				contact_id = contact.get("id").toString();
				if(!seen_ids.contains(contact_id))
				{
					seen_ids.add(contact_id);
					row = Map();
					row.put("contact_id",contact_id);
					row.put("name",ifnull(contact.get("Full_Name"),""));
					row.put("email",ifnull(contact.get("Email"),""));
					row.put("phone",ifnull(contact.get("Phone"),""));
					account_ref = contact.get("Account_Name");
					if(account_ref != null)
					{
						row.put("account_id",ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString());
						row.put("account_name",ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),""));
					}
					matches.add(row);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	out.put("matches",matches);
	input.Request_Status = "done";
}

if(action_name == "get_customer")
{
	contact_id_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	if(contact_id_text != "")
	{
		contact = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Contacts",contact_id_text.toLong());
		if(contact != null)
		{
			out.put("contact_id",contact_id_text);
			out.put("first_name",ifnull(contact.get("First_Name"),""));
			out.put("last_name",ifnull(contact.get("Last_Name"),""));
			out.put("name",ifnull(contact.get("Full_Name"),""));
			out.put("email",ifnull(contact.get("Email"),""));
			out.put("phone",ifnull(contact.get("Phone"),""));
			mailing = Map();
			mailing.put("street",ifnull(contact.get("Mailing_Street"),""));
			mailing.put("city",ifnull(contact.get("Mailing_City"),""));
			mailing.put("state",ifnull(contact.get("Mailing_State"),""));
			mailing.put("code",ifnull(contact.get("Mailing_Zip"),""));
			mailing.put("country",ifnull(contact.get("Mailing_Country"),""));
			out.put("mailing_address",mailing);
			account_ref = contact.get("Account_Name");
			if(account_ref != null)
			{
				account_id_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString();
				out.put("account_id",account_id_text);
				out.put("account_name",ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),""));
				if(account_id_text != "")
				{
					account = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Accounts",account_id_text.toLong());
					if(account != null)
					{
						billing = Map();
						billing.put("street",ifnull(account.get("Billing_Street"),""));
						billing.put("city",ifnull(account.get("Billing_City"),""));
						billing.put("state",ifnull(account.get("Billing_State"),""));
						billing.put("code",ifnull(account.get("Billing_Code"),""));
						billing.put("country",ifnull(account.get("Billing_Country"),""));
						out.put("billing_address",billing);
						shipping = Map();
						shipping.put("street",ifnull(account.get("Shipping_Street"),""));
						shipping.put("city",ifnull(account.get("Shipping_City"),""));
						shipping.put("state",ifnull(account.get("Shipping_State"),""));
						shipping.put("code",ifnull(account.get("Shipping_Code"),""));
						shipping.put("country",ifnull(account.get("Shipping_Country"),""));
						out.put("shipping_address",shipping);
					}
				}
			}
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		else
		{
			out.put("error","Contact " + contact_id_text + " not found");
		}
	}
	else
	{
		out.put("error","get_customer requires a Contact ID in Query_Text");
	}
}

if(action_name == "create_customer")
{
	payload = Map();
	payload_text = ifnull(input.Payload_JSON,"");
	if(payload_text != "")
	{
		payload = payload_text.toMap();
	}
	email_text = ifnull(payload.get("email"),"").trim();
	last_name_text = ifnull(payload.get("last_name"),"").trim();
	if(last_name_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","create_customer requires last_name");
	}
	else
	{
		existing_contact_id = "";
		if(email_text != "")
		{
			dup = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Contacts","(Email:equals:" + email_text + ")");
			if(dup != null)
			{
				if(dup.size() > 0)
				{
					existing_contact_id = dup.get(0).get("id").toString();
				}
			}
		}
		if(existing_contact_id != "")
		{
			out.put("duplicate",true);
			out.put("contact_id",existing_contact_id);
			out.put("message","A CRM Contact with this email already exists; reusing it.");
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		else
		{
			account_id_text = "";
			company_text = ifnull(payload.get("company"),"").trim();
			if(company_text != "")
			{
				acc_found = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Accounts","(Account_Name:equals:" + company_text + ")");
				if(acc_found != null)
				{
					if(acc_found.size() > 0)
					{
						account_id_text = acc_found.get(0).get("id").toString();
					}
				}
				if(account_id_text == "")
				{
					account_data = Map();
					account_data.put("Account_Name",company_text);
					account_data.put("Billing_Street",ifnull(payload.get("billing_street"),""));
					account_data.put("Billing_City",ifnull(payload.get("billing_city"),""));
					account_data.put("Billing_State",ifnull(payload.get("billing_state"),""));
					account_data.put("Billing_Code",ifnull(payload.get("billing_code"),""));
					account_data.put("Billing_Country",ifnull(payload.get("billing_country"),""));
					new_account = zoho.crm.createRecord("Accounts",account_data);
					if(new_account != null)
					{
						account_id_text = ifnull(new_account.get("id"),"").toString();
					}
				}
			}
			contact_data = Map();
			contact_data.put("First_Name",ifnull(payload.get("first_name"),""));
			contact_data.put("Last_Name",last_name_text);
			if(email_text != "")
			{
				contact_data.put("Email",email_text);
			}
			contact_data.put("Phone",ifnull(payload.get("phone"),""));
			contact_data.put("Mailing_Street",ifnull(payload.get("billing_street"),""));
			contact_data.put("Mailing_City",ifnull(payload.get("billing_city"),""));
			contact_data.put("Mailing_State",ifnull(payload.get("billing_state"),""));
			contact_data.put("Mailing_Zip",ifnull(payload.get("billing_code"),""));
			contact_data.put("Mailing_Country",ifnull(payload.get("billing_country"),""));
			if(account_id_text != "")
			{
				contact_data.put("Account_Name",account_id_text);
			}
			new_contact = zoho.crm.createRecord("Contacts",contact_data);
			if(new_contact != null)
			{
				out.put("contact_id",ifnull(new_contact.get("id"),"").toString());
				out.put("account_id",account_id_text);
				out.put("duplicate",false);
				input.Request_Status = "done";
			}
			else
			{
				out.put("error","CRM Contact creation returned null");
			}
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "sync_quote_to_crm")
{
	quote_id_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	if(quote_id_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","sync_quote_to_crm requires a Quote_Request record ID in Query_Text");
	}
	else
	{
		try
		{
			thisapp.fn_calc_quote_lines(quote_id_text);
			thisapp.fn_sync_to_crm(quote_id_text);
			deal_id_out = "";
			for each  quote_hdr in Quote_Request[ID == quote_id_text.toLong()]
			{
				deal_id_out = ifnull(quote_hdr.CRM_Deal_ID,"").toString();
				break;
			}
			out.put("status","synced");
			out.put("quote_id",quote_id_text);
			out.put("crm_deal_id",deal_id_out);
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		catch (sync_err)
		{
			out.put("error","sync_quote_to_crm failed: " + sync_err.toString());
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "get_quote_lines")
{
	quote_id_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	if(quote_id_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","get_quote_lines requires a Quote_Request record ID in Query_Text");
	}
	else
	{
		lines = List();
		found_quote = false;
		for each  quote in Quote_Request[ID == quote_id_text.toLong()]
		{
			found_quote = true;
			for each  line in quote.Quote_Lines
			{
				row = Map();
				row.put("part_number",ifnull(line.Part_Number,""));
				row.put("description",ifnull(line.Description,""));
				row.put("qty",ifnull(line.Qty,0));
				row.put("unit_price",ifnull(line.Unit_Price,""));
				row.put("fx_unit_price",ifnull(line.FX_Unit_Price,""));
				row.put("line_total_usd",ifnull(line.Line_Total_USD,""));
				row.put("line_total_fx",ifnull(line.Line_Total_FX,""));
				row.put("kit_warning",ifnull(line.Kit_Warning,""));
				lines.add(row);
			}
			break;
		}
		if(found_quote)
		{
			out.put("lines",lines);
			for each  quote_hdr in Quote_Request[ID == quote_id_text.toLong()]
			{
				out.put("crm_deal_id",ifnull(quote_hdr.CRM_Deal_ID,""));
				out.put("crm_contact_id",ifnull(quote_hdr.CRM_Contact_ID,""));
				out.put("sign_request_id",ifnull(quote_hdr.Sign_Request_ID,""));
				out.put("status",ifnull(quote_hdr.Status,""));
				break;
			}
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		else
		{
			out.put("error","Quote_Request " + quote_id_text + " not found");
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "books_diag")
{
	org_resp = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/books/v3/organizations"
		type :GET
		connection:"zoho_books_connection"
	];
	orgs = org_resp.get("organizations");
	org_list = List();
	if(orgs != null)
	{
		for each  org in orgs
		{
			om = Map();
			om.put("organization_id",ifnull(org.get("organization_id"),""));
			om.put("name",ifnull(org.get("name"),""));
			om.put("contact_name",ifnull(org.get("contact_name"),""));
			om.put("country",ifnull(org.get("country_code"),ifnull(org.get("country"),"")));
			om.put("currency_code",ifnull(org.get("currency_code"),""));
			om.put("time_zone",ifnull(org.get("time_zone"),""));
			om.put("is_default_org",ifnull(org.get("is_default_org"),""));
			om.put("sales_tax_type",ifnull(org.get("sales_tax_type"),""));
			om.put("tax_group_enabled",ifnull(org.get("tax_group_enabled"),""));
			om.put("version",ifnull(org.get("version"),""));
			org_list.add(om);
		}
	}
	out.put("organizations",org_list);
	first_org_id = "";
	if(org_list.size() > 0)
	{
		first_org_id = org_list.get(0).get("organization_id");
	}
	if(first_org_id != "")
	{
		taxes_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/books/v3/settings/taxes?organization_id=" + first_org_id
			type :GET
			connection:"zoho_books_connection"
		];
		out.put("taxes",ifnull(taxes_resp.get("taxes"),List()));
		auth_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/books/v3/settings/taxauthorities?organization_id=" + first_org_id
			type :GET
			connection:"zoho_books_connection"
		];
		out.put("tax_authorities",ifnull(auth_resp.get("tax_authorities"),List()));
	}
	input.Request_Status = "done";
}

if(action_name == "get_tax")
{
	org_resp = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/books/v3/organizations"
		type :GET
		connection:"zoho_books_connection"
	];
	orgs = org_resp.get("organizations");
	org_id = "";
	org_count = 0;
	if(orgs != null)
	{
		org_count = orgs.size();
		if(org_count == 1)
		{
			org_id = ifnull(orgs.get(0).get("organization_id"),"");
		}
	}
	if(org_id == "")
	{
		if(org_count > 1)
		{
			out.put("error","Tax unavailable -- multiple Books organizations returned (" + org_count + "); explicit organization selection required (see docs/TAX_AUTOMATION_PLAN.md)");
		}
		else
		{
			out.put("error","Tax unavailable -- Zoho Books returned no organization via zoho_books_connection");
		}
		out.put("tax_status","not_connected");
		out.put("source","zoho_books");
	}
	else
	{
		taxes_resp = invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/books/v3/settings/taxes?organization_id=" + org_id
			type :GET
			connection:"zoho_books_connection"
		];
		tax_list = ifnull(taxes_resp.get("taxes"),List());
		out.put("organization_id",org_id);
		out.put("configured_taxes",tax_list);
		out.put("source","zoho_books");
		out.put("tax_status","insufficient_permissions");
		out.put("error","Tax unavailable -- read-only tax list cannot produce an address-specific rate; Sales Tax Automation calculates at transaction level. Smallest additional permission: Estimates -> Create (draft-estimate probe). See docs/TAX_AUTOMATION_PLAN.md.");
	}
}

if(action_name == "expand_kit")
{
	payload = Map();
	payload_text = ifnull(input.Payload_JSON,"");
	if(payload_text != "")
	{
		payload = payload_text.toMap();
	}
	kit_key_text = ifnull(payload.get("kit_key"),"").toString().trim();
	series_cells_text = ifnull(payload.get("series_cell_count"),"").toString().trim();
	if(kit_key_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","expand_kit requires kit_key in Payload_JSON");
	}
	else
	{
		series_cells_valid = true;
		series_cells = 0;
		if(series_cells_text != "")
		{
			if(series_cells_text.isNumber())
			{
				series_cells = series_cells_text.toLong();
			}
			else
			{
				series_cells_valid = false;
			}
		}
		if(!series_cells_valid)
		{
			out.put("error","expand_kit: series_cell_count must be a whole number (got \"" + series_cells_text + "\")");
		}
		else
		{
			resp = thisapp.fn_get_kit_components(kit_key_text,series_cells);
			err = ifnull(resp.get("error"),"").toString();
			if(err != "")
			{
				out.put("error",err);
			}
			else
			{
				lines = List();
				resp_lines = resp.get("lines");
				if(resp_lines != null)
				{
					for each  line in resp_lines
					{
						row = Map();
						row.put("part_number",ifnull(line.get("Part_Number"),""));
						row.put("qty",ifnull(line.get("Qty"),0));
						row.put("kit_warning",ifnull(line.get("Kit_Warning"),""));
						lines.add(row);
					}
				}
				out.put("kit_key",kit_key_text);
				out.put("lines",lines);
				out.put("notices",ifnull(resp.get("notices"),List()));
				input.Request_Status = "done";
			}
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "search_deals")
{
	payload = Map();
	payload_text = ifnull(input.Payload_JSON,"");
	if(payload_text != "")
	{
		payload = payload_text.toMap();
	}
	contact_id_text = ifnull(payload.get("contact_id"),"").toString().trim();
	account_id_text = ifnull(payload.get("account_id"),"").toString().trim();
	if(contact_id_text == "" && account_id_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","search_deals requires contact_id and/or account_id in Payload_JSON");
	}
	else
	{
		closed_stages = List();
		closed_stages.add("Closed Won");
		closed_stages.add("Closed Lost");
		closed_stages.add("Closed Lost to Competition");
		deal_rows = List();
		seen_deal_ids = List();
		related_sets = List();
		if(contact_id_text != "")
		{
			contact_deals = zoho.crm.getRelatedRecords("Deals","Contacts",contact_id_text.toLong());
			if(contact_deals != null)
			{
				related_sets.add(contact_deals);
			}
		}
		if(account_id_text != "")
		{
			account_deals = zoho.crm.getRelatedRecords("Deals","Accounts",account_id_text.toLong());
			if(account_deals != null)
			{
				related_sets.add(account_deals);
			}
		}
		for each  related_list in related_sets
		{
			for each  deal in related_list
			{
				deal_id_val = ifnull(deal.get("id"),"").toString();
				if(deal_id_val != "")
				{
					if(!seen_deal_ids.contains(deal_id_val))
					{
						seen_deal_ids.add(deal_id_val);
						stage_val = ifnull(deal.get("Stage"),"").toString();
						row = Map();
						row.put("deal_id",deal_id_val);
						row.put("deal_name",ifnull(deal.get("Deal_Name"),""));
						row.put("stage",stage_val);
						row.put("amount",ifnull(deal.get("Amount"),""));
						row.put("modified_time",ifnull(deal.get("Modified_Time"),"").toString());
						account_ref = deal.get("Account_Name");
						if(account_ref != null)
						{
							row.put("account_name",ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),""));
						}
						contact_ref = deal.get("Contact_Name");
						if(contact_ref != null)
						{
							row.put("contact_name",ifnull(contact_ref.get("name"),""));
						}
						is_closed = false;
						if(closed_stages.contains(stage_val))
						{
							is_closed = true;
						}
						row.put("is_closed",is_closed);
						deal_rows.add(row);
					}
				}
			}
		}
		out.put("deals",deal_rows);
		input.Request_Status = "done";
	}
}

if(action_name == "get_deal")
{
	deal_id_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	if(deal_id_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","get_deal requires a CRM Deal record ID in Query_Text");
	}
	else
	{
		deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",deal_id_text.toLong());
		if(deal == null)
		{
			out.put("error","Deal " + deal_id_text + " not found");
		}
		else
		{
			closed_stages = List();
			closed_stages.add("Closed Won");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost to Competition");
			stage_val = ifnull(deal.get("Stage"),"").toString();
			out.put("deal_id",deal_id_text);
			out.put("deal_name",ifnull(deal.get("Deal_Name"),""));
			out.put("stage",stage_val);
			out.put("amount",ifnull(deal.get("Amount"),""));
			out.put("modified_time",ifnull(deal.get("Modified_Time"),"").toString());
			account_ref = deal.get("Account_Name");
			if(account_ref != null)
			{
				out.put("account_name",ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),""));
			}
			contact_ref = deal.get("Contact_Name");
			if(contact_ref != null)
			{
				out.put("contact_name",ifnull(contact_ref.get("name"),""));
			}
			is_closed = false;
			if(closed_stages.contains(stage_val))
			{
				is_closed = true;
			}
			out.put("is_closed",is_closed);
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "create_deal")
{
	payload = Map();
	payload_text = ifnull(input.Payload_JSON,"");
	if(payload_text != "")
	{
		payload = payload_text.toMap();
	}
	deal_name_text = ifnull(payload.get("deal_name"),"").toString().trim();
	contact_id_text = ifnull(payload.get("contact_id"),"").toString().trim();
	account_id_text = ifnull(payload.get("account_id"),"").toString().trim();
	account_name_text = ifnull(payload.get("account_name"),"").toString().trim();
	if(deal_name_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","create_deal requires deal_name in Payload_JSON");
	}
	else
	{
		deal_data = Map();
		deal_data.put("Deal_Name",deal_name_text);
		deal_data.put("Stage","Proposal/Price Quote");
		deal_data.put("Closing_Date",zoho.currentdate.addDay(30).toString("yyyy-MM-dd"));
		if(account_id_text != "")
		{
			deal_data.put("Account_Name",account_id_text);
		}
		else if(account_name_text != "")
		{
			deal_data.put("Account_Name",account_name_text);
		}
		if(contact_id_text != "")
		{
			deal_data.put("Contact_Name",contact_id_text);
		}
		new_deal = zoho.crm.createRecord("Deals",deal_data);
		created_id = "";
		if(new_deal != null)
		{
			created_id = ifnull(new_deal.get("id"),"").toString();
		}
		if(created_id != "")
		{
			out.put("deal_id",created_id);
			out.put("deal_name",deal_name_text);
			out.put("stage","Proposal/Price Quote");
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
		else
		{
			out.put("error","CRM Deal creation returned null");
		}
	}
}

if(action_name == "search_leads")
{
	query_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	lead_matches = List();
	seen_lead_ids = List();
	if(query_text != "")
	{
		lead_criteria = "(Email:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Last_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(First_Name:starts_with:" + query_text + ")or(Company:starts_with:" + query_text + ")";
		has_space = query_text.contains(" ");
		if(has_space)
		{
			name_tokens = query_text.toList(" ");
			token_count = name_tokens.size();
			first_token = name_tokens.get(0).trim();
			last_index = token_count - 1;
			last_token = name_tokens.get(last_index).trim();
			if(first_token != "" && last_token != "")
			{
				lead_criteria = lead_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + "))";
				lead_criteria = lead_criteria + "or((First_Name:starts_with:" + last_token + ")and(Last_Name:starts_with:" + first_token + "))";
			}
		}
		found_leads = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Leads",lead_criteria);
		if(found_leads != null)
		{
			for each  lead in found_leads
			{
				lead_id_val = ifnull(lead.get("id"),"").toString();
				if(lead_id_val != "")
				{
					if(!seen_lead_ids.contains(lead_id_val))
					{
						is_converted = ifnull(lead.get("$converted"),false);
						if(is_converted == false)
						{
							seen_lead_ids.add(lead_id_val);
							row = Map();
							row.put("lead_id",lead_id_val);
							row.put("name",ifnull(lead.get("Full_Name"),""));
							row.put("email",ifnull(lead.get("Email"),""));
							row.put("phone",ifnull(lead.get("Phone"),""));
							row.put("company",ifnull(lead.get("Company"),""));
							lead_matches.add(row);
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	out.put("lead_matches",lead_matches);
	input.Request_Status = "done";
}

if(action_name == "get_lead")
{
	lead_id_text = ifnull(input.Query_Text,"").trim();
	if(lead_id_text == "")
	{
		out.put("error","get_lead requires a CRM Lead ID in Query_Text");
	}
	else
	{
		lead_rec = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Leads",lead_id_text.toLong());
		if(lead_rec == null)
		{
			out.put("error","CRM Lead " + lead_id_text + " not found");
		}
		else
		{
			out.put("lead_id",lead_id_text);
			out.put("name",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Full_Name"),""));
			out.put("email",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Email"),""));
			out.put("phone",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Phone"),""));
			out.put("company",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Company"),""));
			lead_addr = Map();
			lead_addr.put("street",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Street"),""));
			lead_addr.put("city",ifnull(lead_rec.get("City"),""));
			lead_addr.put("state",ifnull(lead_rec.get("State"),""));
			lead_addr.put("code",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Zip_Code"),""));
			lead_addr.put("country",ifnull(lead_rec.get("Country"),""));
			out.put("address",lead_addr);
			input.Request_Status = "done";
		}
	}
}

known_actions = List();
known_actions.add("search_customers");
known_actions.add("get_customer");
known_actions.add("create_customer");
known_actions.add("sync_quote_to_crm");
known_actions.add("get_quote_lines");
known_actions.add("expand_kit");
known_actions.add("get_tax");
known_actions.add("books_diag");
known_actions.add("search_deals");
known_actions.add("create_deal");
known_actions.add("get_deal");
known_actions.add("search_leads");
known_actions.add("get_lead");
if(!known_actions.contains(action_name))
{
	out.put("error","Unknown Action: " + action_name);
}

input.Result_jSON = out.toString();

# FILE: workflows/expand_kit_button.deluge

// Expand Kit record-button workflow on Quote_Request. Plan in
// docs/PHASE_C_DELUGE_PLAN.md. Inserts Quote_Lines rows only; pricing is
// delegated to save-time fn_calc_quote_lines (unchanged).
kit_key = ifnull(input.Kit_Select,"").toString().trim();
if(kit_key == "")
{
	input.Kit_Expand_Notice = "Pick a kit in Kit Select before expanding.";
}
else
{
	kit_main_sku = "";
	for each  comp in Kit_Components[Kit_Key == kit_key]
	{
		req = ifnull(comp.Requirement,"").toString();
		if(req == "main")
		{
			kit_main_sku = ifnull(comp.Component_SKU,"").toString();
			break;
		}
	}
	already_expanded = false;
	if(kit_main_sku != "")
	{
		for each  ln in input.Quote_Lines
		{
			pn = ifnull(ln.Part_Number,"").toString().trim();
			if(pn == kit_main_sku)
			{
				already_expanded = true;
			}
		}
	}
	if(kit_main_sku == "")
	{
		input.Kit_Expand_Notice = "Kit data error: no main row found for kit " + kit_key + ".";
	}
	else if(already_expanded)
	{
		input.Kit_Expand_Notice = "Kit already expanded on this quote (main SKU " + kit_main_sku + " present). No rows added.";
	}
	else
	{
		series_cells = ifnull(input.Series_Cell_Count,0);
		resp = thisapp.fn_get_kit_components(kit_key,series_cells);
		err = ifnull(resp.get("error"),"").toString();
		if(err != "")
		{
			input.Kit_Expand_Notice = err;
		}
		else
		{
			inserted_count = 0;
			resp_lines = resp.get("lines");
			for each  line in resp_lines
			{
				line_pn = line.get("Part_Number");
				line_qty = line.get("Qty");
				line_warn = line.get("Kit_Warning");
				nr = Quote_Request.Quote_Lines();
				nr.Part_Number = line_pn;
				nr.Qty = line_qty;
				nr.Kit_Warning = line_warn;
				input.Quote_Lines.insert(nr);
				inserted_count = inserted_count + 1;
			}
			notice_text = "Kit " + kit_key + " expanded: " + inserted_count + " lines added.";
			resp_notices = resp.get("notices");
			for each  n in resp_notices
			{
				notice_text = notice_text + "\n" + n;
			}
			notice_text = notice_text + "\nSave the quote to price the inserted lines.";
			input.Kit_Expand_Notice = notice_text;
		}
	}
}

================================================================================
# FILE: workflows/on_user_input_quote_currency_fx.deluge
================================================================================
currency = ifnull(input.Currency,"USD");
fx_mode = ifnull(input.FX_Charge_Mode,"non_usd_only");
apply_fx = false;
fx_rate = 1.0;

fx_mode_always = false;
if(fx_mode == "always")
{
	fx_mode_always = true;
}

fx_mode_non_usd_only = false;
if(fx_mode == "non_usd_only")
{
	fx_mode_non_usd_only = true;
}

currency_not_usd = false;
if(currency != "USD")
{
	currency_not_usd = true;
}

if(fx_mode_always)
{
	apply_fx = true;
}
if(fx_mode_non_usd_only)
{
	if(currency_not_usd)
	{
		apply_fx = true;
	}
}

fx_rate_found = false;
if(apply_fx)
{
	for each rate_rec in FX_Rates_Cache[Currency == currency]
	{
		fx_rate = ifnull(rate_rec.Rate,0);
		break;
	}
	if(fx_rate != 0)
	{
		fx_rate_found = true;
	}
}

fx_missing = false;
if(apply_fx)
{
	if(fx_rate_found == false)
	{
		fx_missing = true;
	}
}

for each ln in input.Quote_Lines
{
	unit_price_usd = ifnull(ln.Unit_Price, 0);
	line_total_usd = ifnull(ln.Line_Total_USD, 0);

	fx_applies = false;
	if(apply_fx)
	{
		if(fx_rate_found)
		{
			fx_applies = true;
		}
	}

	if(fx_applies)
	{
		ln.FX_Unit_Price = (unit_price_usd * fx_rate).round(2);
		ln.Line_Total_FX = (line_total_usd * fx_rate).round(2);
	}
	else
	{
		if(fx_missing)
		{
			ln.FX_Unit_Price = 0;
			ln.Line_Total_FX = 0;
			desc_now = ifnull(ln.Description,"");
			has_fx_marker = desc_now.contains("[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE");
			if(has_fx_marker == false)
			{
				ln.Description = "[FX RATE UNAVAILABLE FOR " + currency + " - DO NOT QUOTE] " + desc_now;
			}
		}
		else
		{
			ln.FX_Unit_Price = unit_price_usd.round(2);
			ln.Line_Total_FX = line_total_usd.round(2);
		}
	}
}

# FILE: scripts/qts_publish/advance_deal_stage.deluge
================================================================================
map advance_deal_stage(string deal_id, map deal_record)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("stage_updated","no");
result.put("target_stage","Negotiation/Review");
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_id_missing");
	return result;
}
target_stage = "Negotiation/Review";
stage_rank = Map();
stage_rank.put("Qualification",1);
stage_rank.put("Needs Analysis",2);
stage_rank.put("Value Proposition",3);
stage_rank.put("Identify Decision Makers",4);
stage_rank.put("Id. Decision Makers",4);
stage_rank.put("Proposal/Price Quote",5);
stage_rank.put("Negotiation/Review",6);
stage_rank.put("Closed Won",99);
stage_rank.put("Closed Lost",99);
stage_rank.put("Closed Lost to Competition",99);
try
{
	deal = deal_record;
	if(deal == null || deal.isEmpty())
	{
		deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	}
	if(deal == null)
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","deal_not_found");
		return result;
	}
	current_stage = ifnull(deal.get("Stage"),"").toString().trim();
	cur_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(current_stage),0).toLong();
	tgt_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(target_stage),6).toLong();
	if(cur_rank >= 99)
	{
		result.put("status","ok");
		result.put("reason","terminal_deal_not_reopened");
		result.put("current_stage",current_stage);
		return result;
	}
	if(cur_rank >= tgt_rank)
	{
		result.put("status","ok");
		result.put("reason","already_at_or_past_target");
		result.put("current_stage",current_stage);
		return result;
	}
	upd = Map();
	upd.put("Stage",target_stage);
	resp = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),upd);
	if(resp != null && ifnull(resp.get("status"),"") != "error")
	{
		result.put("status","ok");
		result.put("stage_updated","yes");
		result.put("previous_stage",current_stage);
	}
	else
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","stage_update_failed");
	}
}
catch (stage_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","stage_update_exception");
}
return result;
}

map assert_deal_has_products(string deal_id, map deal_record)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("product_count",0);
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_id_missing");
	return result;
}
quoted_count = 0;
try
{
	deal = deal_record;
	if(deal == null || deal.isEmpty())
	{
		deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	}
	if(deal != null)
	{
		products = ifnull(deal.get("Associated_Products"),List());
		for each item in products
		{
			quantity = ifnull(item.get("Quantity"),0).toDecimal();
			product = item.get("Product");
			if(quantity > 0 && product != null && ifnull(product.get("id"),"").toString() != "")
			{
				quoted_count = quoted_count + 1;
			}
		}
	}
}
catch (deal_read_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_read_failed");
	return result;
}
result.put("product_count",quoted_count);
if(quoted_count == 0)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","associated_products_empty_after_sync");
	return result;
}
result.put("status","ok");
return result;
}

# FILE: scripts/qts_publish/crm_replace_deal_attachment.deluge
================================================================================
map crm_replace_deal_attachment(string deal_id, file merged_file, string quote_number, map deal_record)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("attached_to_deal","no");
result.put("prior_attachments_deleted",0);
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
safe_qno = ifnull(quote_number,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "" || merged_file == null)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_or_file_missing");
	return result;
}
// Delete prior Kinetic Bridge quote PDFs on the Deal so only one current attach remains.
try
{
	att_resp = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments"
		type :GET
		connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
	];
	atts = ifnull(att_resp.get("data"),List());
	delete_ids = "";
	deleted_n = 0;
	for each att in atts
	{
		fname = ifnull(att.get("File_Name"),"").toString();
		aid = ifnull(att.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
		match = false;
		if(fname.contains("Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_"))
		{
			match = true;
		}
		if(!match && safe_qno != "" && fname.contains(safe_qno) && fname.toLowerCase().endsWith(".pdf"))
		{
			match = true;
		}
		if(match && aid != "")
		{
			if(delete_ids != "")
			{
				delete_ids = delete_ids + ",";
			}
			delete_ids = delete_ids + aid;
			deleted_n = deleted_n + 1;
		}
	}
	if(delete_ids != "")
	{
		invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments?ids=" + delete_ids
			type :DELETE
			connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
		];
		result.put("prior_attachments_deleted",deleted_n);
	}
}
catch (prior_attach_error)
{
	result.put("prior_attach_delete","skipped");
}
try
{
	deal_attach = zoho.crm.attachFile("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),merged_file,"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow");
	if(deal_attach != null && ifnull(deal_attach.get("status"),"") != "error")
	{
		result.put("attached_to_deal","yes");
		result.put("status","ok");
	}
	else
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","deal_attach_failed");
	}
}
catch (attach_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_attach_exception");
}
if(safe_qno != "" && result.get("status") == "ok")
{
	try
	{
		deal = deal_record;
		if(deal == null || deal.isEmpty())
		{
			deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
		}
		company_name_text = "";
		if(deal != null)
		{
			account_ref = deal.get("Account_Name");
			if(account_ref != null)
			{
				company_name_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),"").toString().trim();
			}
			if(company_name_text == "")
			{
				company_name_text = ifnull(deal.get("Deal_Name"),"").toString().trim();
			}
		}
		quote_suffix_marker = " - TEST-QUOTE";
		if(company_name_text.contains(quote_suffix_marker))
		{
			company_name_text = company_name_text.subString(0,company_name_text.indexOf(quote_suffix_marker)).trim();
		}
		if(company_name_text.contains(" - "))
		{
			parts = company_name_text.toList(" - ");
			if(parts.size() > 1 && parts.get(parts.size() - 1).contains("QUOTE"))
			{
				company_name_text = parts.get(0).trim();
			}
		}
		if(company_name_text == "") { company_name_text = "Quote"; }
		deal_name_update = Map();
		deal_name_update.put("Deal_Name",company_name_text + " - " + safe_qno);
		zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),deal_name_update);
		result.put("deal_renamed","yes");
	}
	catch (rename_error)
	{
		result.put("deal_renamed","no");
	}
}
return result;
}

map ensure_deal_contact_link(string deal_id, string contact_id, map deal_record)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("updated","no");
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
safe_contact_id = ifnull(contact_id,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "" || safe_contact_id == "")
{
	result.put("reason","deal_or_contact_missing");
	return result;
}
try
{
	deal = deal_record;
	if(deal == null || deal.isEmpty())
	{
		deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	}
	if(deal == null)
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","deal_not_found");
		return result;
	}
	contact_ref = deal.get("Contact_Name");
	existing_id = "";
	if(contact_ref != null)
	{
		existing_id = ifnull(contact_ref.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
	}
	if(existing_id == safe_contact_id)
	{
		result.put("status","ok");
		result.put("reason","already_linked");
		return result;
	}
	upd = Map();
	contact_lookup = Map();
	contact_lookup.put("id",safe_contact_id);
	upd.put("Contact_Name",contact_lookup);
	resp = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),upd);
	if(resp != null && ifnull(resp.get("status"),"") != "error")
	{
		result.put("status","ok");
		result.put("updated","yes");
	}
	else
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","contact_link_update_failed");
	}
}
catch (link_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","contact_link_exception");
}
return result;
}

merge_params.put("merge_data",merge_payload.toString());
merge_params.put("output_settings","{\"format\":\"pdf\"}");
merge_params.put("filename","Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + quote_number);
merged_file = invokeurl
[
	url : "https://writer.zoho.com/writer/api/v1/documents/" + template_document_id + "/merge"
	type : POST
	parameters : merge_params
	connection : "writer_to_flow"
];
if(merged_file == null)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","merge_failed");
	return result;
}
try
{
	err_msg = ifnull(merged_file.get("error"),"").toString().trim();
	err_code = ifnull(merged_file.get("code"),"").toString().trim();
	err_message = ifnull(merged_file.get("message"),"").toString().trim();
	if(err_msg != "" || err_code != "" || err_message != "")
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","merge_error_payload");
		return result;
	}
}
catch (not_error_map)
{
}
safe_contact_id = ifnull(contact_id,"").trim();
quote_record_id = "";
quoted_items = List();
deal_owner_email = "";
account_id_text = "";
company_name_text = ifnull(merge_data.get("company_name"),"").toString().trim();
try
{
	deal_record = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	if(deal_record != null)
	{
		owner_ref = deal_record.get("Owner");
		if(owner_ref != null)
		{
			deal_owner_email = ifnull(owner_ref.get("email"),"").toString().trim();
		}
		account_ref = deal_record.get("Account_Name");
		if(account_ref != null)
		{
			account_id_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
			if(company_name_text == "")
			{
				company_name_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),"").toString().trim();
			}
		}
		if(company_name_text == "")
		{
			company_name_text = ifnull(deal_record.get("Deal_Name"),"").toString().trim();
		}
		products = ifnull(deal_record.get("Associated_Products"),List());
		for each item in products
		{
			quantity = ifnull(item.get("Quantity"),0).toDecimal();
			if(quantity > 0)
			{
				product = item.get("Product");
				if(product != null && ifnull(product.get("id"),"").toString() != "")
				{
					product_lookup = Map();
					product_lookup.put("id",ifnull(product.get("id"),"").toString());
					quoted_item = Map();
					quoted_item.put("Product_Name",product_lookup);
					quoted_item.put("Quantity",quantity);
					quoted_item.put("List_Price",ifnull(item.get("List_Price"),0).toDecimal());
					quoted_items.add(quoted_item);
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
catch (deal_refetch_error)
{
	quoted_items = List();
}
if(quoted_items.size() == 0)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","associated_products_empty_after_sync");
	return result;
}
if(send_email && quoted_items.size() > 0)
{
	quote_subject = "Kinetic Bridge Quote " + quote_number;
	quote_map = Map();
	quote_map.put("Subject",quote_subject.trim());
	deal_lookup = Map();
	deal_lookup.put("id",safe_deal_id);
	quote_map.put("Deal_Name",deal_lookup);
	if(safe_contact_id != "")
	{
		quote_contact_lookup = Map();
		quote_contact_lookup.put("id",safe_contact_id);
		quote_map.put("Contact_Name",quote_contact_lookup);
	}
	valid_till_value = ifnull(merge_data.get("valid_till"),"").toString().trim();
	if(valid_till_value != "")
	{
		try
		{
			quote_map.put("Valid_Till",valid_till_value.toDate().toString("yyyy-MM-dd"));
		}
		catch (valid_till_error)
		{
		}
	}
	safe_quote_request_id = ifnull(quote_request_id,"").trim();
	if(safe_quote_request_id != "")
	{
		quote_map.put("Description","QTS Quote_Request ID: " + safe_quote_request_id);
	}
	quote_map.put("Quoted_Items",quoted_items);
	existing_quote_id = "";
	try
	{
		existing_quotes = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Quotes","(Subject:equals:" + quote_subject.trim() + ")");
		if(existing_quotes != null && existing_quotes.size() > 0)
		{
			existing_quote_id = ifnull(existing_quotes.get(0).get("id"),"").toString().trim();
		}
	}
	catch (quote_search_error)
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","quote_search_failed");
		return result;
	}
	if(existing_quote_id != "")
	{
		try
		{
			quote_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Quotes",existing_quote_id.toLong(),quote_map);
			if(quote_response != null && ifnull(quote_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
			{
				quote_record_id = existing_quote_id;
				result.put("quote_action","updated");
			}
		}
		catch (quote_update_error)
		{
			quote_record_id = "";
		}
	}
	else
	{
		try
		{
			quote_response = zoho.crm.createRecord("Quotes",quote_map);
			if(quote_response != null)
			{
				quote_record_id = ifnull(quote_response.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
				if(quote_record_id != "")
				{
					result.put("quote_action","created");
				}
			}
		}
		catch (quote_create_error)
		{
			quote_record_id = "";
		}
	}
}
if(quote_record_id != "")
{
	try
	{
		quote_attach = zoho.crm.attachFile("Quotes",quote_record_id.toLong(),merged_file,"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow");
		if(quote_attach != null && ifnull(quote_attach.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("attached_to_quote","yes");
			result.put("quote_record_id",quote_record_id);
		}
	}
	catch (quote_attach_error)
	{
		result.put("attached_to_quote","no");
	}
}
try
{
	att_resp = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments"
		type :GET
		connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
	];
	atts = ifnull(att_resp.get("data"),List());
	delete_ids = "";
	for each att in atts
	{
		fname = ifnull(att.get("File_Name"),"").toString();
		aid = ifnull(att.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
		if(aid != "" && fname.contains("Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_"))
		{
			if(delete_ids != "") { delete_ids = delete_ids + ","; }
			delete_ids = delete_ids + aid;
		}
	}
	if(delete_ids != "")
	{
		invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments?ids=" + delete_ids
			type :DELETE
			connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
		];
	}
}
catch (prior_attach_error)
{
}
try
{
	deal_attach = zoho.crm.attachFile("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),merged_file,"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow");
	if(deal_attach != null && ifnull(deal_attach.get("status"),"") != "error")
	{
		result.put("attached_to_deal","yes");
	}
	else
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","deal_attach_failed");
		return result;
	}
}
catch (deal_attach_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_attach_exception");
	return result;
}
if(safe_contact_id != "" && quote_number != "")
{
	contact_record = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Contacts",safe_contact_id.toLong());
	if(contact_record != null && ifnull(contact_record.get("First_Quote_Number"),"") == "")
	{
		offset_raw = zoho.currenttime.toString("Z");
		stamp = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss") + offset_raw.subString(0,3) + ":" + offset_raw.subString(3,5);
		stamp_fields = Map();
		stamp_fields.put("First_Quote_Number",quote_number);
		stamp_fields.put("First_Quote_Created_At",stamp);
		stamp_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Contacts",safe_contact_id.toLong(),stamp_fields);
		if(stamp_response != null && ifnull(stamp_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("first_quote_stamped","yes");
		}
	}
}
if(quote_number != "" && safe_deal_id != "")
{
	deal_label_company = company_name_text;
	quote_suffix_marker = " - TEST-QUOTE";
	if(deal_label_company.contains(quote_suffix_marker))
	{
		deal_label_company = deal_label_company.subString(0,deal_label_company.indexOf(quote_suffix_marker)).trim();
	}
	if(deal_label_company == "")
	{
		deal_label_company = "Quote";
	}
	deal_name_update = Map();
	deal_name_update.put("Deal_Name",deal_label_company + " - " + quote_number);
	try
	{
		deal_rename_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),deal_name_update);
		if(deal_rename_response != null && ifnull(deal_rename_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("deal_renamed","yes");
			result.put("deal_name",deal_label_company + " - " + quote_number);
		}
	}
	catch (deal_rename_error)
	{
		result.put("deal_renamed","no");
	}
}
if(send_email && safe_deal_id != "")
{
	try
	{
		stage_deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
		if(stage_deal != null)
		{
			cur_stage = ifnull(stage_deal.get("Stage"),"").toString().trim();
			closed_stages = List();
			closed_stages.add("Closed Won");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost to Competition");
			stage_rank = Map();
			stage_rank.put("Qualification",1);
			stage_rank.put("Needs Analysis",2);
			stage_rank.put("Value Proposition",3);
			stage_rank.put("Identify Decision Makers",4);
			stage_rank.put("Id. Decision Makers",4);
			stage_rank.put("Proposal/Price Quote",5);
			stage_rank.put("Negotiation/Review",6);
			if(!closed_stages.contains(cur_stage))
			{
				cur_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(cur_stage),0);
				if(cur_rank < 6)
				{
					stage_upd = Map();
					stage_upd.put("Stage","Negotiation/Review");
					stage_resp = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),stage_upd);
					if(stage_resp != null && ifnull(stage_resp.get("status"),"") != "error")
					{
						result.put("deal_stage_advanced","yes");
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	catch (stage_advance_error)
	{
		result.put("deal_stage_advanced","no");
	}
}
safe_contact_email = ifnull(contact_email,"").trim();
if(send_email && safe_contact_email != "")
{
	is_revision = result.get("quote_action") == "updated";
	email_subject = "Your Kinetic Bridge quote " + quote_number;
	intro_line = "Thank you for your interest in Kinetic Bridge. Please find your quote " + quote_number + " attached.";
	if(is_revision)
	{
		email_subject = "Your updated Kinetic Bridge quote " + quote_number;
		intro_line = "Please find the updated version of your quote " + quote_number + " attached. This replaces the previous version.";
	}
	email_body = "Hello,<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + intro_line + "<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + "The quote is valid until the date shown on the document. Reply to this email with any questions and we will be glad to help.<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + "Best regards,<br>The Kinetic Bridge Team<br>a BEVCO company";
	mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
	if(deal_owner_email != "")
	{
		mail_from = deal_owner_email;
	}
	quote_cc = ifnull(cc_email,"").trim();
	if(quote_cc == "")
	{
		quote_cc = "info@kinetic-bridge.com";
	}
	try
	{
		sendmail
		[
			from : mail_from
			to : safe_contact_email
			cc : quote_cc
			subject : email_subject
			message : email_body
			Attachments : file:merged_file
		]
		result.put("email_sent","yes");
		result.put("email_from",mail_from);
	}
	catch (owner_send_error)
	{
		sendmail
		[
			from : zoho.adminuserid
			to : safe_contact_email
			cc : quote_cc
			subject : email_subject
			message : email_body
			Attachments : file:merged_file
		]
		result.put("email_sent","yes");
		result.put("email_from","admin_fallback");
		mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
	}
	if(result.get("email_sent") == "yes")
	{
		assoc_message_id = "qts-quote-" + quote_number + "-" + zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
		assoc_date_time = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX","America/Los_Angeles");
		from_item = Map();
		from_item.put("user_name",mail_from);
		from_item.put("email",mail_from.toString().trim().toLowerCase());
		to_item = Map();
		to_item.put("user_name",safe_contact_email);
		to_item.put("email",safe_contact_email.toLowerCase());
		to_list = List();
		to_list.add(to_item);
		email_item = Map();
		email_item.put("from",from_item);
		email_item.put("to",to_list);
		email_item.put("subject",email_subject);
		email_item.put("content",email_body);
		email_item.put("mail_format","html");
		email_item.put("date_time",assoc_date_time);
		email_item.put("sent",true);
		email_item.put("original_message_id",assoc_message_id);
		if(quote_cc != "")
		{
			cc_list = List();
			cc_item = Map();
			cc_item.put("user_name",quote_cc);
			cc_item.put("email",quote_cc.toLowerCase());
			cc_list.add(cc_item);
			email_item.put("cc",cc_list);
		}
		emails = List();
		emails.add(email_item);
		request_body = Map();
		request_body.put("Emails",emails);
		if(safe_contact_id != "")
		{
			try
			{
				assoc_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Contacts/" + safe_contact_id + "/actions/associate_email"
					type :POST
					parameters:request_body.toString()
					connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
				];
				assoc_code = "";
				assoc_status = "";
				assoc_items = assoc_response.get("Emails");
				if(assoc_items != null && assoc_items.size() > 0)
				{
					assoc_row = assoc_items.get(0);
					assoc_code = ifnull(assoc_row.get("code"),"").toString();
					assoc_status = ifnull(assoc_row.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				else
				{
					assoc_code = ifnull(assoc_response.get("code"),"").toString();
					assoc_status = ifnull(assoc_response.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				if(assoc_code == "SUCCESS" || assoc_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA" || assoc_status == "success")
				{
					result.put("email_associated","yes");
				}
			}
			catch (assoc_contact_error)
			{
				result.put("email_associated","no");
			}
		}
		if(account_id_text != "")
		{
			try
			{
				assoc_account_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Accounts/" + account_id_text + "/actions/associate_email"
					type :POST
					parameters:request_body.toString()
					connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
				];
				acct_code = "";
				acct_status = "";
				acct_items = assoc_account_response.get("Emails");
				if(acct_items != null && acct_items.size() > 0)
				{
					acct_row = acct_items.get(0);
					acct_code = ifnull(acct_row.get("code"),"").toString();
					acct_status = ifnull(acct_row.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				else
				{
					acct_code = ifnull(assoc_account_response.get("code"),"").toString();
					acct_status = ifnull(assoc_account_response.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				if(acct_code == "SUCCESS" || acct_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA" || acct_status == "success")
				{
					result.put("email_associated_account","yes");
				}
			}
			catch (assoc_account_error)
			{
				result.put("email_associated_account","no");
			}
		}
	}
}
result.put("status","filed");
result.put("reason","");
return result;
}

# FILE: scripts/qts_publish/generate_and_file_quote_document.deluge
================================================================================
map generate_and_file_quote_document(string deal_id, string contact_id, string contact_email, map merge_data, bool send_email, string quote_request_id, string cc_email)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("reason","");
result.put("attached_to_deal","no");
result.put("attached_to_quote","no");
result.put("quote_record_id","");
result.put("quote_action","");
result.put("first_quote_stamped","no");
result.put("email_sent","no");
result.put("email_associated","no");
result.put("email_associated_account","no");
result.put("deal_renamed","no");
branch = "save";
if(send_email)
{
	branch = "email";
}
result.put("branch",branch);
template_document_id = "h5zhu2051759ecefd4148816cf61ad65a9cbc";
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "")
{
	result.put("reason","deal_id_missing");
	return result;
}
if(merge_data == null || merge_data.isEmpty())
{
	result.put("reason","merge_data_missing");
	return result;
}
quote_number = ifnull(merge_data.get("quote_number"),"").toString();
data_list = List();
data_list.add(merge_data);
merge_payload = Map();
merge_payload.put("data",data_list);
merge_params = Map();
merge_params.put("merge_data",merge_payload.toString());
merge_params.put("output_settings","{\"format\":\"pdf\"}");
merge_params.put("filename","Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + quote_number);
merged_file = invokeurl
[
	url : "https://writer.zoho.com/writer/api/v1/documents/" + template_document_id + "/merge"
	type : POST
	parameters : merge_params
	connection : "writer_to_flow"
];
if(merged_file == null)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","merge_failed");
	return result;
}
try
{
	err_msg = ifnull(merged_file.get("error"),"").toString().trim();
	err_code = ifnull(merged_file.get("code"),"").toString().trim();
	err_message = ifnull(merged_file.get("message"),"").toString().trim();
	if(err_msg != "" || err_code != "" || err_message != "")
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","merge_error_payload");
		return result;
	}
}
catch (not_error_map)
{
}
safe_contact_id = ifnull(contact_id,"").trim();
quote_record_id = "";
quoted_items = List();
deal_owner_email = "";
account_id_text = "";
company_name_text = ifnull(merge_data.get("company_name"),"").toString().trim();
try
{
	deal_record = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	if(deal_record != null)
	{
		owner_ref = deal_record.get("Owner");
		if(owner_ref != null)
		{
			deal_owner_email = ifnull(owner_ref.get("email"),"").toString().trim();
		}
		account_ref = deal_record.get("Account_Name");
		if(account_ref != null)
		{
			account_id_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
			if(company_name_text == "")
			{
				company_name_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("name"),"").toString().trim();
			}
		}
		if(company_name_text == "")
		{
			company_name_text = ifnull(deal_record.get("Deal_Name"),"").toString().trim();
		}
		products = ifnull(deal_record.get("Associated_Products"),List());
		for each item in products
		{
			quantity = ifnull(item.get("Quantity"),0).toDecimal();
			if(quantity > 0)
			{
				product = item.get("Product");
				if(product != null && ifnull(product.get("id"),"").toString() != "")
				{
					product_lookup = Map();
					product_lookup.put("id",ifnull(product.get("id"),"").toString());
					quoted_item = Map();
					quoted_item.put("Product_Name",product_lookup);
					quoted_item.put("Quantity",quantity);
					quoted_item.put("List_Price",ifnull(item.get("List_Price"),0).toDecimal());
					quoted_items.add(quoted_item);
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
catch (deal_refetch_error)
{
	quoted_items = List();
}
if(quoted_items.size() == 0)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","associated_products_empty_after_sync");
	return result;
}
if(send_email && quoted_items.size() > 0)
{
	quote_subject = "Kinetic Bridge Quote " + quote_number;
	quote_map = Map();
	quote_map.put("Subject",quote_subject.trim());
	deal_lookup = Map();
	deal_lookup.put("id",safe_deal_id);
	quote_map.put("Deal_Name",deal_lookup);
	if(safe_contact_id != "")
	{
		quote_contact_lookup = Map();
		quote_contact_lookup.put("id",safe_contact_id);
		quote_map.put("Contact_Name",quote_contact_lookup);
	}
	valid_till_value = ifnull(merge_data.get("valid_till"),"").toString().trim();
	if(valid_till_value != "")
	{
		try
		{
			quote_map.put("Valid_Till",valid_till_value.toDate().toString("yyyy-MM-dd"));
		}
		catch (valid_till_error)
		{
		}
	}
	safe_quote_request_id = ifnull(quote_request_id,"").trim();
	if(safe_quote_request_id != "")
	{
		quote_map.put("Description","QTS Quote_Request ID: " + safe_quote_request_id);
	}
	quote_map.put("Quoted_Items",quoted_items);
	existing_quote_id = "";
	try
	{
		existing_quotes = zoho.crm.searchRecords("Quotes","(Subject:equals:" + quote_subject.trim() + ")");
		if(existing_quotes != null && existing_quotes.size() > 0)
		{
			existing_quote_id = ifnull(existing_quotes.get(0).get("id"),"").toString().trim();
		}
	}
	catch (quote_search_error)
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","quote_search_failed");
		return result;
	}
	if(existing_quote_id != "")
	{
		try
		{
			quote_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Quotes",existing_quote_id.toLong(),quote_map);
			if(quote_response != null && ifnull(quote_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
			{
				quote_record_id = existing_quote_id;
				result.put("quote_action","updated");
			}
		}
		catch (quote_update_error)
		{
			quote_record_id = "";
		}
	}
	else
	{
		try
		{
			quote_response = zoho.crm.createRecord("Quotes",quote_map);
			if(quote_response != null)
			{
				quote_record_id = ifnull(quote_response.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
				if(quote_record_id != "")
				{
					result.put("quote_action","created");
				}
			}
		}
		catch (quote_create_error)
		{
			quote_record_id = "";
		}
	}
}
if(send_email && quote_record_id != "")
{
	try
	{
		quote_attach = zoho.crm.attachFile("Quotes",quote_record_id.toLong(),merged_file,"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow");
		if(quote_attach != null && ifnull(quote_attach.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("attached_to_quote","yes");
			result.put("quote_record_id",quote_record_id);
		}
	}
	catch (quote_attach_error)
	{
		result.put("attached_to_quote","no");
	}
}
try
{
	att_resp = invokeurl
	[
		url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments"
		type :GET
		connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
	];
	atts = ifnull(att_resp.get("data"),List());
	delete_ids = "";
	for each att in atts
	{
		fname = ifnull(att.get("File_Name"),"").toString();
		aid = ifnull(att.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
		if(aid != "" && fname.contains("Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_"))
		{
			if(delete_ids != "") { delete_ids = delete_ids + ","; }
			delete_ids = delete_ids + aid;
		}
	}
	if(delete_ids != "")
	{
		invokeurl
		[
			url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v2/Deals/" + safe_deal_id + "/Attachments?ids=" + delete_ids
			type :DELETE
			connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
		];
	}
}
catch (prior_attach_error)
{
}
try
{
	deal_attach = zoho.crm.attachFile("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),merged_file,"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow");
	if(deal_attach != null && ifnull(deal_attach.get("status"),"") != "error")
	{
		result.put("attached_to_deal","yes");
	}
	else
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","deal_attach_failed");
		return result;
	}
}
catch (deal_attach_error)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_attach_exception");
	return result;
}
if(safe_contact_id != "" && quote_number != "")
{
	contact_record = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Contacts",safe_contact_id.toLong());
	if(contact_record != null && ifnull(contact_record.get("First_Quote_Number"),"") == "")
	{
		offset_raw = zoho.currenttime.toString("Z");
		stamp = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss") + offset_raw.subString(0,3) + ":" + offset_raw.subString(3,5);
		stamp_fields = Map();
		stamp_fields.put("First_Quote_Number",quote_number);
		stamp_fields.put("First_Quote_Created_At",stamp);
		stamp_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Contacts",safe_contact_id.toLong(),stamp_fields);
		if(stamp_response != null && ifnull(stamp_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("first_quote_stamped","yes");
		}
	}
}
if(quote_number != "" && safe_deal_id != "")
{
	deal_label_company = company_name_text;
	quote_suffix_marker = " - TEST-QUOTE";
	if(deal_label_company.contains(quote_suffix_marker))
	{
		deal_label_company = deal_label_company.subString(0,deal_label_company.indexOf(quote_suffix_marker)).trim();
	}
	if(deal_label_company == "")
	{
		deal_label_company = "Quote";
	}
	deal_name_update = Map();
	deal_name_update.put("Deal_Name",deal_label_company + " - " + quote_number);
	try
	{
		deal_rename_response = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),deal_name_update);
		if(deal_rename_response != null && ifnull(deal_rename_response.get("status"),"") != "error")
		{
			result.put("deal_renamed","yes");
			result.put("deal_name",deal_label_company + " - " + quote_number);
		}
	}
	catch (deal_rename_error)
	{
		result.put("deal_renamed","no");
	}
}
if(send_email && safe_deal_id != "")
{
	try
	{
		stage_deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
		if(stage_deal != null)
		{
			cur_stage = ifnull(stage_deal.get("Stage"),"").toString().trim();
			closed_stages = List();
			closed_stages.add("Closed Won");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost");
			closed_stages.add("Closed Lost to Competition");
			stage_rank = Map();
			stage_rank.put("Qualification",1);
			stage_rank.put("Needs Analysis",2);
			stage_rank.put("Value Proposition",3);
			stage_rank.put("Identify Decision Makers",4);
			stage_rank.put("Id. Decision Makers",4);
			stage_rank.put("Proposal/Price Quote",5);
			stage_rank.put("Negotiation/Review",6);
			if(!closed_stages.contains(cur_stage))
			{
				cur_rank = ifnull(stage_rank.get(cur_stage),0);
				if(cur_rank < 6)
				{
					stage_upd = Map();
					stage_upd.put("Stage","Negotiation/Review");
					stage_resp = zoho.crm.updateRecord("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong(),stage_upd);
					if(stage_resp != null && ifnull(stage_resp.get("status"),"") != "error")
					{
						result.put("deal_stage_advanced","yes");
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	catch (stage_advance_error)
	{
		result.put("deal_stage_advanced","no");
	}
}
safe_contact_email = ifnull(contact_email,"").trim();
if(send_email && safe_contact_email != "")
{
	is_revision = result.get("quote_action") == "updated";
	email_subject = "Your Kinetic Bridge quote " + quote_number;
	intro_line = "Thank you for your interest in Kinetic Bridge. Please find your quote " + quote_number + " attached.";
	if(is_revision)
	{
		email_subject = "Your updated Kinetic Bridge quote " + quote_number;
		intro_line = "Please find the updated version of your quote " + quote_number + " attached. This replaces the previous version.";
	}
	email_body = "Hello,<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + intro_line + "<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + "The quote is valid until the date shown on the document. Reply to this email with any questions and we will be glad to help.<br><br>";
	email_body = email_body + "Best regards,<br>The Kinetic Bridge Team<br>a BEVCO company";
	mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
	if(deal_owner_email != "")
	{
		mail_from = deal_owner_email;
	}
	quote_cc = ifnull(cc_email,"").trim();
	if(quote_cc == "")
	{
		quote_cc = "info@kinetic-bridge.com";
	}
	try
	{
		sendmail
		[
			from : mail_from
			to : safe_contact_email
			cc : quote_cc
			subject : email_subject
			message : email_body
			Attachments : file:merged_file
		]
		result.put("email_sent","yes");
		result.put("email_from",mail_from);
	}
	catch (owner_send_error)
	{
		sendmail
		[
			from : zoho.adminuserid
			to : safe_contact_email
			cc : quote_cc
			subject : email_subject
			message : email_body
			Attachments : file:merged_file
		]
		result.put("email_sent","yes");
		result.put("email_from","admin_fallback");
		mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
	}
	if(result.get("email_sent") == "yes")
	{
		assoc_message_id = "qts-quote-" + quote_number + "-" + zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
		assoc_date_time = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX","America/Los_Angeles");
		from_item = Map();
		from_item.put("user_name",mail_from);
		from_item.put("email",mail_from.toString().trim().toLowerCase());
		to_item = Map();
		to_item.put("user_name",safe_contact_email);
		to_item.put("email",safe_contact_email.toLowerCase());
		to_list = List();
		to_list.add(to_item);
		email_item = Map();
		email_item.put("from",from_item);
		email_item.put("to",to_list);
		email_item.put("subject",email_subject);
		email_item.put("content",email_body);
		email_item.put("mail_format","html");
		email_item.put("date_time",assoc_date_time);
		email_item.put("sent",true);
		email_item.put("original_message_id",assoc_message_id);
		if(quote_cc != "")
		{
			cc_list = List();
			cc_item = Map();
			cc_item.put("user_name",quote_cc);
			cc_item.put("email",quote_cc.toLowerCase());
			cc_list.add(cc_item);
			email_item.put("cc",cc_list);
		}
		emails = List();
		emails.add(email_item);
		request_body = Map();
		request_body.put("Emails",emails);
		if(safe_contact_id != "")
		{
			try
			{
				assoc_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Contacts/" + safe_contact_id + "/actions/associate_email"
					type :POST
					parameters:request_body.toString()
					connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
				];
				assoc_code = "";
				assoc_status = "";
				assoc_items = assoc_response.get("Emails");
				if(assoc_items != null && assoc_items.size() > 0)
				{
					assoc_row = assoc_items.get(0);
					assoc_code = ifnull(assoc_row.get("code"),"").toString();
					assoc_status = ifnull(assoc_row.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				else
				{
					assoc_code = ifnull(assoc_response.get("code"),"").toString();
					assoc_status = ifnull(assoc_response.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				if(assoc_code == "SUCCESS" || assoc_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA" || assoc_status == "success")
				{
					result.put("email_associated","yes");
				}
			}
			catch (assoc_contact_error)
			{
				result.put("email_associated","no");
			}
		}
		if(account_id_text != "")
		{
			try
			{
				assoc_account_response = invokeurl
				[
					url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Accounts/" + account_id_text + "/actions/associate_email"
					type :POST
					parameters:request_body.toString()
					connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
				];
				acct_code = "";
				acct_status = "";
				acct_items = assoc_account_response.get("Emails");
				if(acct_items != null && acct_items.size() > 0)
				{
					acct_row = acct_items.get(0);
					acct_code = ifnull(acct_row.get("code"),"").toString();
					acct_status = ifnull(acct_row.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				else
				{
					acct_code = ifnull(assoc_account_response.get("code"),"").toString();
					acct_status = ifnull(assoc_account_response.get("status"),"").toString();
				}
				if(acct_code == "SUCCESS" || acct_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA" || acct_status == "success")
				{
					result.put("email_associated_account","yes");
				}
			}
			catch (assoc_account_error)
			{
				result.put("email_associated_account","no");
			}
		}
	}
}
result.put("status","filed");
result.put("reason","");
return result;
}

================================================================================
# FILE: scripts/qts_publish/merge_quote_pdf.deluge
================================================================================
map merge_quote_pdf(map merge_data, string quote_number)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("reason","");
result.put("merged_file",null);
template_document_id = "h5zhu2051759ecefd4148816cf61ad65a9cbc";
if(merge_data == null || merge_data.isEmpty())
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","merge_data_missing");
	return result;
}
safe_qno = ifnull(quote_number,"").trim();
if(safe_qno == "")
{
	safe_qno = ifnull(merge_data.get("quote_number"),"").toString().trim();
}
data_list = List();
data_list.add(merge_data);
merge_payload = Map();
merge_payload.put("data",data_list);
merge_params = Map();
merge_params.put("merge_data",merge_payload.toString());
merge_params.put("output_settings","{\"format\":\"pdf\"}");
merge_params.put("filename","Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + safe_qno);
merged_file = invokeurl
[
	url : "https://writer.zoho.com/writer/api/v1/documents/" + template_document_id + "/merge"
	type : POST
	parameters : merge_params
	connection : "writer_to_flow"
];
if(merged_file == null)
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","merge_failed");
	return result;
}
// Writer error payloads arrive as maps with error/code — reject before filing/emailing.
try
{
	err_msg = ifnull(merged_file.get("error"),"").toString().trim();
	err_code = ifnull(merged_file.get("code"),"").toString().trim();
	err_message = ifnull(merged_file.get("message"),"").toString().trim();
	if(err_msg != "" || err_code != "" || err_message != "")
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","merge_error_payload");
		return result;
	}
}
catch (not_error_map)
{
}
result.put("status","ok");
result.put("merged_file",merged_file);
result.put("filename","Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + safe_qno + ".pdf");
return result;
}

================================================================================
# FILE: scripts/qts_publish/publish_quote_package.deluge
================================================================================
map publish_quote_package(string deal_id, string contact_id, string contact_email, map merge_data, bool send_email, string quote_request_id, string cc_email, string workdrive_parent_folder_id)
{
result = Map();
result.put("status","skipped");
result.put("send_email",send_email);
branch = "save";
if(send_email)
{
	branch = "email";
}
result.put("branch",branch);
safe_deal_id = ifnull(deal_id,"").trim();
if(safe_deal_id == "")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","deal_id_missing");
	return result;
}
if(merge_data == null || merge_data.isEmpty())
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","merge_data_missing");
	return result;
}
quote_number = ifnull(merge_data.get("quote_number"),"").toString().trim();
if(quote_number == "")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","quote_number_missing");
	return result;
}
stable_filename = "Kinetic_Bridge_Quote_" + quote_number + ".pdf";
revision = 1;
try
{
	revision = ifnull(merge_data.get("revision"),1).toLong();
}
catch (rev_error)
{
	revision = 1;
}
if(revision < 1)
{
	revision = 1;
}
// Single Deal fetch for the whole publish run — pass into helpers to avoid External Calls amplification.
deal_record = Map();
try
{
	deal_record = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
	if(deal_record == null)
	{
		deal_record = Map();
	}
}
catch (deal_fetch_error)
{
	deal_record = Map();
}
products_check = assert_deal_has_products(safe_deal_id,deal_record);
result.put("assert_products",products_check);
if(ifnull(products_check.get("status"),"") != "ok")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason",ifnull(products_check.get("reason"),"associated_products_empty_after_sync"));
	return result;
}
link_check = ensure_deal_contact_link(safe_deal_id,ifnull(contact_id,"").trim(),deal_record);
result.put("deal_contact_link",link_check);
merge_res = merge_quote_pdf(merge_data,quote_number);
result.put("merge",merge_res);
if(ifnull(merge_res.get("status"),"") != "ok")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason",ifnull(merge_res.get("reason"),"merge_failed"));
	return result;
}
merged_file = merge_res.get("merged_file");
folders = workdrive_ensure_quote_folders(quote_number,ifnull(workdrive_parent_folder_id,"").trim());
result.put("workdrive_folders",folders);
if(ifnull(folders.get("status"),"") == "ok")
{
	archive_res = workdrive_archive_to_drafts(ifnull(folders.get("current_id"),"").toString(),ifnull(folders.get("drafts_id"),"").toString(),stable_filename,revision,"",false);
	result.put("workdrive_archive",archive_res);
	store_cur = workdrive_store_current(ifnull(folders.get("current_id"),"").toString(),merged_file,stable_filename);
	result.put("workdrive_current",store_cur);
	if(send_email)
	{
		archive_conf = workdrive_archive_to_drafts("",ifnull(folders.get("drafts_id"),"").toString(),stable_filename,revision,ifnull(folders.get("confirmed_id"),"").toString(),true);
		result.put("workdrive_archive_confirmed",archive_conf);
		store_conf = workdrive_store_confirmed(ifnull(folders.get("confirmed_id"),"").toString(),merged_file,stable_filename);
		result.put("workdrive_confirmed",store_conf);
	}
}
else
{
	result.put("workdrive_skipped_reason",ifnull(folders.get("reason"),"workdrive_unavailable"));
}
deal_attach = crm_replace_deal_attachment(safe_deal_id,merged_file,quote_number,deal_record);
result.put("deal_attachment",deal_attach);
if(ifnull(deal_attach.get("status"),"") != "ok")
{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason",ifnull(deal_attach.get("reason"),"deal_attach_failed"));
	return result;
}
try
{
	snap = snapshot_creator_revision(ifnull(quote_request_id,"").trim(),quote_number,merge_data,folders);
	result.put("creator_revision",snap);
}
catch (snap_error)
{
	result.put("creator_revision_error","skipped");
}
if(send_email)
{
	quote_res = upsert_crm_quote(safe_deal_id,ifnull(contact_id,"").trim(),quote_number,ifnull(quote_request_id,"").trim(),merge_data,merged_file,deal_record);
	result.put("crm_quote",quote_res);
	if(ifnull(quote_res.get("status"),"") != "ok")
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason",ifnull(quote_res.get("reason"),"quote_upsert_failed"));
		return result;
	}
	mail_res = send_quote_email(safe_deal_id,ifnull(contact_id,"").trim(),ifnull(contact_email,"").trim(),quote_number,ifnull(quote_res.get("quote_action"),""),merged_file,ifnull(cc_email,"").trim(),deal_record);
	result.put("email",mail_res);
	stage_res = advance_deal_stage(safe_deal_id,deal_record);
	result.put("deal_stage",stage_res);
}
result.put("status","filed");
result.put("reason","");
return result;
}

map send_quote_email(string deal_id, string contact_id, string contact_email, string quote_number, string quote_action, file merged_file, string cc_email, map deal_record)
{
result = Map();

result.put("email_sent","no");
result.put("email_associated","no");
result.put("email_associated_account","no");

safe_qno = ifnull(quote_number,"").trim();

{
	result.put("status","failed");
	result.put("reason","contact_email_or_file_missing");
	return result;

account_id_text = "";

if(safe_deal_id != "")
{
	try
	{
		deal = deal_record;
		if(deal == null || deal.isEmpty())
		{
			deal = zoho.crm.getRecordById("Deals",safe_deal_id.toLong());
		}
		if(deal != null)

			owner_ref = deal.get("Owner");
			if(owner_ref != null)

			account_ref = deal.get("Account_Name");
			if(account_ref != null)
			{
				account_id_text = ifnull(account_ref.get("id"),"").toString().trim();
			}
		}

	catch (deal_ref_error)

	}
}
is_revision = ifnull(quote_action,"") == "updated";

intro_line = "Thank you for your interest in Kinetic Bridge. Please find your quote " + safe_qno + " attached.";
if(is_revision)
{
	email_subject = "Your updated Kinetic Bridge quote " + safe_qno;
	intro_line = "Please find the updated version of your quote " + safe_qno + " attached. This replaces the previous version.";
}
email_body = "Hello,<br><br>";
email_body = email_body + intro_line + "<br><br>";
email_body = email_body + "The quote is valid until the date shown on the document. Reply to this email with any questions and we will be glad to help.<br><br>";
email_body = email_body + "Best regards,<br>The Kinetic Bridge Team<br>a BEVCO company";
mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
if(deal_owner_email != "")
{
	mail_from = deal_owner_email;
}
quote_cc = ifnull(cc_email,"").trim();
if(quote_cc == "")
{
	quote_cc = "info@kinetic-bridge.com";
}
try
{
	sendmail
	[
		from : mail_from
		to : safe_contact_email
		cc : quote_cc
		subject : email_subject
		message : email_body
		Attachments : file:merged_file
	]
	result.put("email_sent","yes");
	result.put("email_from",mail_from);
}
catch (owner_send_error)
{
	try
	{
		sendmail
		[
			from : zoho.adminuserid
			to : safe_contact_email
			cc : quote_cc
			subject : email_subject
			message : email_body
			Attachments : file:merged_file
		]
		result.put("email_sent","yes");
		result.put("email_from","admin_fallback");
		mail_from = zoho.adminuserid;
	}
	catch (admin_send_error)
	{
		result.put("status","failed");
		result.put("reason","email_send_failed");
		return result;
	}
}
if(result.get("email_sent") == "yes")
{
	assoc_message_id = "qts-quote-" + safe_qno + "-" + zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyyMMddHHmmss");
	assoc_date_time = zoho.currenttime.toString("yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ssXXX","America/Los_Angeles");
	from_item = Map();
	from_item.put("user_name",mail_from);
	from_item.put("email",mail_from.toString().trim().toLowerCase());
	to_item = Map();
	to_item.put("user_name",safe_contact_email);
	to_item.put("email",safe_contact_email.toLowerCase());
	to_list = List();
	to_list.add(to_item);
	email_item = Map();
	email_item.put("from",from_item);
	email_item.put("to",to_list);
	email_item.put("subject",email_subject);
	email_item.put("content",email_body);
	email_item.put("mail_format","html");
	email_item.put("date_time",assoc_date_time);
	email_item.put("sent",true);
	email_item.put("original_message_id",assoc_message_id);
	if(quote_cc != "")
	{
		cc_list = List();
		cc_item = Map();
		cc_item.put("user_name",quote_cc);
		cc_item.put("email",quote_cc.toLowerCase());
		cc_list.add(cc_item);
		email_item.put("cc",cc_list);
	}
	emails = List();
	emails.add(email_item);
	request_body = Map();
	request_body.put("Emails",emails);
	safe_contact_id = ifnull(contact_id,"").trim();
	if(safe_contact_id != "")
	{
		try
		{
			assoc_response = invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Contacts/" + safe_contact_id + "/actions/associate_email"
				type :POST
				parameters:request_body.toString()
				connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
			];
			assoc_code = "";
			assoc_status = "";
			assoc_items = assoc_response.get("Emails");
			if(assoc_items != null && assoc_items.size() > 0)
			{
				assoc_row = assoc_items.get(0);
				assoc_code = ifnull(assoc_row.get("code"),"").toString();
				assoc_status = ifnull(assoc_row.get("status"),"").toString();
			}
			if(assoc_code == "SUCCESS" || assoc_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA" || assoc_status == "success")
			{
				result.put("email_associated","yes");
			}
		}
		catch (assoc_contact_error)
		{
			result.put("email_associated","no");
		}
	}
	if(account_id_text != "")
	{
		try
		{
			assoc_account_response = invokeurl
			[
				url :"https://www.zohoapis.com/crm/v8/Accounts/" + account_id_text + "/actions/associate_email"
				type :POST
				parameters:request_body.toString()
				connection:"zoho_crm_to_zoho_flow"
			];
			acct_code = "";
			acct_items = assoc_account_response.get("Emails");
			if(acct_items != null && acct_items.size() > 0)
			{
				acct_row = acct_items.get(0);
				acct_code = ifnull(acct_row.get("code"),"").toString();
			}
			if(acct_code == "SUCCESS" || acct_code == "DUPLICATE_DATA")
			{
				result.put("email_associated_account","yes");
			}
		}
		catch (assoc_account_error)
		{
			result.put("email_associated_account","no");
		}
	}
}
result.put("status","ok");
return result;
}
